       1. puṟam. 27.

   cutatta muṉivaṉ tavam ceytiruttal
   itattiṉai yuyirkkiṉi tavittiṭu miyaṟkaic
       cutattamuṉi tottiru viḷaittukaḷ tuṭaikkum *
       matattayaṉ matakkaḷi ṟeṉappaṭima niṟpak
       katattiṉa ṉiḻittaṭu+ cuṭattiṭai maṭuttāṉ.         263
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *(ḷuṭaikkum.) +katattuṭaṉiḻittaṭu.

   urai:- uyirkku-ellāvuyirkaṭkum, itattiṉai iṉitu aḷittiṭum,
   iyaṟkai-nalattai mikacceyyum iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya, cutattamuṉi-cutattaṉ
   eṉṉum muṉivaṉākiya, tottu iruviṉaittakaḷ-uyiraippaṟṟi niṟkum
   iruviṉaikaḷākiya māciṉai, tuṭaikkum-pōkkum,
   patattuayaṉ-tiruvaṭiyaiyuṭaiya mēlōṉuṭaiya, paṭimam-tiruvuruvam,
   matakkaḷiṟu eṉa niṟpa-matam paṭṭa kaḷiṟṟiṉaippōlac calippuṇarātu
   niṟkakkaṇṭu, katattiṉaṉ-kōpattai yuṭaiyaṉākiya aracaṉ, iḻittu- ikaḻntu
   nōkki, avaṉirunta, aṭukaṭattiṭai-tāṉ uyirkaḷaik kollak karuti vanta
   kāṭṭiṭattai, maṭuttāṉ- vantaṭaintāṉe-ṟu.
   aracaṉ uyirkaḷaik kollum karuttiṉaṉāy vantāṉeṉpatai muṭittaṟku,
   muṉivaṉatu uyirkkaruḷum iyaṟkaiyai eṭuttu vitantār.
   tottutal-paṟṟiniṟṟal. nalviṉaiyum tuṉpa muṭiviṟ ṟātalpaṟṟi
   "iruviṉaittukaḷ" eṉṟār; "iruḷ cēr iruviṉaiyum cērā"1 eṉṟārpōla.
   tukaḷeṉavē, tuṭaittal viṉai kūṟiṉār. aiyaṉ eṉpatu ayaṉ eṉa vikāram.
   tavattāl poṟipulaṉkaḷai yaṭakkiyiruttal paṟṟi, muṉivaṉ tiruvuruvaip
   paṭimam eṉṟār. calippiṉmaikku matakkaḷiṟu uvamamāyiṟṟu. iṉi ataṉai
   aracaṉmē lēṟṟalu moṉṟu. kaṭam, kāṭu. veḷḷampōlat tāṉai puṭaitiraṇṭu
   varutaliṉ, maṭuttāṉ eṉṟār.
   -----------------
       1 kuṟaḷ 5

   vēntaṉ cutatta muṉivaṉai vekuḷutal
   kūṟṟameṉa aṭavipuṭai taṭaviyuyir kōṟaṟ
       kēṟṟapaṭi peṟṟatila ṉiṟṟaiviṉai muṟṟum
       pāṟṟiyava ṉiṉṉuyir paṟippaleṉa vantāṉ
       māṟṟariya cīṟṟamoṭu mātavaṉiṉ mēlē.         264

   urai:- aṭavi-ikkāṭṭil, puṭaitaṭavi-iṭameṅkum cuṟṟiyum, kūṟṟameṉak
   kōṟaṟku ēṟṟapaṭi-namaṉ pōlak kollutaṟ kēṟṟavakaiyil, peṟṟatu ilaṉ-
   uyiroṉṟum peṟṟēṉillai. iṟṟai viṉai muṟṟum pāṟṟiyavaṉ-iṉṟu eṉatu vēṭṭai
   viṉai muḻutum payaṉiṉṟik keṭacceyta im muṉivaṉuṭaiya, iṉṉuyir
   paṟippal-iṉiya uyiraip pōkkuvēṉ, eṉa-eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭu, māṟṟariya
   cīṟṟamoṭu-nīkka muṭiyāta kōpattuṭaṉē, mātavaṉiṉ mēlē-cutatta
   muṉivaṉmēl, vantāṉ-maṇṭivaralāṉāṉ e-ṟu
   mēlēyum "paṭaikkaṭal naṭuccel namaṉē pōl"(262) eṉṟārākaliṉ, īṇṭum
   avviyalpu tiriyāmaiyiṉ "kūṟṟa meṉa" eṉṟār. ōriṭamum viṭātu tēṭiṉamai
   tōṉṟa, "aṭavipuṭai taṭavi" yeṉṟār. ēṟṟapaṭiyāvatu, palvakai
   uyirkaḷaiyum koṉṟu kuvippēṉeṉak karuttuṭkoṇṭu pōnta mēṟkōḷ. ōruyirum
   taṉakku akappaṭātoḻintataṟkuk kāraṇam ammuṉivaṉatu iruppeṉṟu
   niṉaittaliṉ, muṉivaṉai, "iṟṟai viṉai muṟṟum pāṟṟiyavaṉ" eṉṟum, atu tāṉ
   mēṟ koṇṭa viṉaikku iṭaiyūṟāvatu niṉaintu vekuḷi mikak kōṭaliṉ "iṉṉuyir
   paṟippal" eṉṟum kūṟalāṉāṉ. muṉivaṉatu tavamum taṉ ceyaliṉatu avamum
   ciṟitu eṇṇiya vaḻiyum, uṇṭākiya ciṉam taṇintu paṇivikkum eṉpār,
   "māṟṟariya cīṟṟamoṭu mātavaṉiṉ mēlē" eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
   aiññūṟu nāykaḷai ammuṉivaṉ mēlēvutal
   konteri yumiḻntetir kuraittatirva kōṇāy
       aintiṉoṭu porutatokai yaimpati ṉiraṭṭi
       centacaikaḷ ceṉṟukavar kiṉṟeṉa* viṭuttāṉ
       nantiyaruḷ maḻaipoḻiyum nātaṉavaṉ mēlē.         265
       --------------
       (pāṭam)* keṉṟeṉa; kiṉṉeṉa

   urai:- kontu eri umiḻntu-kottākat tīyiṉaik kakki, etir kuraittu-etirē
   kuraittukkoṇṭu, atirva-ēṉaiyuyirkaṭku naṭukkattaip paṇṇuvaṉavākiya, kōḷ
   nāy-kolaipuriyum nāykkūṭṭam. aintiṉoṭu porutatokai aimpatiṉ
   iraṭṭi-aintāṟ perukkiya nūṟākavarum aiññūṟum, iṉṟu-ippoḻutē, ceṉṟu
   muṉivaṉai yaṇukippaṟṟi, centacaikaḷ kavarka eṉa-(avaṉuṭaiya) cevviya
   tacaikaḷaik kavarka eṉṟu, aruḷ maḻai nanti-aruḷai maḻaipōla mikuttu,
   poḻiyum nātaṉ avaṉmēl-poḻiyum tavamākiya celvattaiyuṭaiya ammuṉivaṉmēl,
   viṭuttāṉ-celuttiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   vēṭṭai nāykaḷiṉ iyalpu kūṟuvār, tīppaṟakkum kaṇṇum, ēṉaiyuyirkaḷai
   naṭuṅkuvikkum kuraippum, kolai vaṉmaiyum uṭaiyaveṉṟār. atirtal,
   naṭukkam; īṇṭup piṟaviṉaip poruṭṭu. aimpatiṉ iraṭṭi tokai-aimpatiṉ
   iraṭṭippākiya tokai, nūṟeṉpatām. aḵtu aintōṭu uṟaḻntavaḻi aiññūṟāyiṟṟu.
   tavattāl muṉivaṉmēṉi poṉpōl oḷiviṭṭut tikaḻvatu kaṇṭu "centacaikaḷ"
   eṉṟum,nāykaḷai ēvutal tōṉṟa, iṉṟu kavarka eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ, ataṉaik
   kaṇṭuvaittum cutatta muṉivaṉ veṟuppō viruppō koḷḷātu aruṇ maṇaṅ kamaḻa
   viruntamai viḷaṅka "nanti yaruṇ maḻai poḻiyum nātaṉavaṉ mēlē" eṉṟu
   vitantār. nātaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ citaivu celvamuṭaiyōṉ eṉṉum poruṭṭu.
   makāmakōpāttiyāya paṇṭitamaṇiyavarkaḷ "nātaṉṟāḷ vāḻka" eṉpataṟ keḻutiya
   uraikāṇka.
   nāykkaṇam mēṟcellāmaikaṇṭu aracaṉ vāḷuruvutal
   aṟapperumai ceytaruḷ tavapperumai taṉṉāl
       uṟappuṇarta lañciyoru viṟkaṇavai niṟpak
       kaṟuppuṭai maṉatteḻu katattaraca ṉaiyō
       maṟappaṭai viṭakkaruti vāḷuruvu kiṉṟāṉ.         266

   urai:- aṟapperumai taṉṉāl-mēṟ koṇṭirukkum caiṉa tarumattiṉ
   perumaiyālum, ceytu aruḷ tavapperumai taṉṉāl-uyirkaṭku nalam ceytaruḷum
   tavattiṉ perumaiyālum, uṟappuṇartal añci-muṉivaṉuṭaiya meyyuṟa
   aṇukuvataṟ kañci, oruviṟkaṇ-oru villaḷavu tolaivilēyayē, avai
   niṟpa-anta nāyk kūṭṭam niṉṟoḻiyavē, kaṟuppuṭai maṉattu-eppōtum vekuḷiyē
   kuṭikoṇṭirukkum maṉattiṉkaṇ, eḻukatattu aracaṉ-eḻunta
   ciṉamikutiyaiyuṭaiya aracaṉākiya yacōmati, maṟappaṭai viṭak
   karuti-kolaiviṉaikkuriya paṭaiyaic celutta niṉaintu, vāḷ
   uruvukiṉṟāṉ-uṟaiyilirukkum vāḷaiyuruvuvāṉāyiṉāṉ, aiyō - ivaṉ
   ceyaliruntavāṟu eṉṉō e-ṟu.
   taṉṉāl eṉpatai aṟapperumaikkum kūṭṭuka. aracaṉuṇarāṉāyiṉum, avaṉ
   viṭutta nāykkūṭṭam muṉivaṉuṭaiya aṟap perumai tavap perumaikaḷait
   teḷiya vuṇarntu koṇṭaṉa eṉpār, "uṟappuṇartal añci" yeṉṟum, avvuṇarviṉ
   payaṉ ōraḷavu vilakiniṉṟu vaḻipaṭuvatākaliṉ, "oruviṟ kaṇ avai niṟpa"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. avaṉuṇarāmaikku ētu avaṉ maṉaviyalpu eṉpār, "kaṟuppuṭai
   maṉattu" eṉṟum, ataṉ payaṉāka, avaṉ ciṉam mikuntu vāḷaiyuruvaluṟṟāṉ
   eṉpār, "eḻukatattaracaṉ maṟappaṭai viṭakkaruti vāḷuruvukiṉṟāṉ" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāreṉavuṇarka. kolaikkurittātaliṉ,vāḷ, "maṟappaṭai" yeṉappaṭṭatu.
   aiyō, nūlāciriyar iraṅkik kūṟiyatu.
   kaliyāṇamittiraṉ aracaṉait taṭuttal
   kāḷaitaku kaliyāṇa mittira ṉeṉumpēr
       āḷiyaṭu tiṟalvaṇika ṉaracaṉuyi raṉaiya
       kēḷoruvaṉ vantiṭaipu kuntaraca keṭṭēṉ
       vāḷuruvu kiṉṟateṉai mātavaṉmu ṉeṉṟāṉ.         267

   urai:-kāḷaitaku- kāḷaipparuvam takkirukkiṉṟa,kaliyāṇamittiraṉ eṉum pēr-
   kaliyāṇamittiraṉ eṉṉum peyaraiyuṭaiya, āḷi aṭutiṟalvaṇikaṉ- ciṅkattai
   vellum meyvaliyiṉaiyuṭaiya vaṇikakumaraṉum, aracaṉ - vēntaṉākiya
   yacōmatikku, uyir aṉaiya kēḷ oruvaṉ - uyirotta naṇpaṉumākiya oruvaṉ,
   van tu - avviṭattiṟku vantu, iṭaipu kuntu - vāḷuruvi vīcakkarutum
   araṉait taṭuttu, araca- vēntē, mātavaṉmuṉ- immāmuṉikaḷiṉ tirumuṉṉē, vāḷ
   uruvukiṉṟatu eṉai - tīṅku kuṟittu vāṭpaṭai yeṭuppatu eṉṉaiyō e-ṟu.
   keṭṭēṉ, irakkakkuṟippu. kaliyāṇamittiraṉuṭaiya mati nuṭpamum, mey
   vaṉmaiyum, aracaṉpāl avaṟkuḷḷa toṭarpum viḷakkutaṟku, "kāḷai" yeṉṟum,
   "āḷiyaṭutiṟal vaṇikaṉ" eṉṟum, "aracaṉ uyiraṉaiya kēḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   oppaṟṟa tuṇaivaṉ eṉpār "oruvaṉ" eṉṟār; mikacciṟan tāṉ eṉpatu karuttu;
   iṉṟēl, iṭaipukuntu vilakkutal iyalāteṉavuṇarka. acceyalāl viḷaiyum
   kēṭṭiṉai aṟintu pōntu vilakkukiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, iṭai pukuntu "keṭṭēṉ" eṉṟu
   colleṭuttu, "vāḷuruvukiṉṟa teṉai mātavaṉ muṉ" eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. mātavaṉ
   eṉṟatu, nī karutiyavāṟu muṭikka voṇṇātu taṭukkum perumaiyum, nī karutum
   karuttu niṉṉaiyē yaṭaivikkum tavavaṉmaiyum uṭaiyaṉ eṉpatu viḷaṅkak
   kūṟiyavāṟām.
   kaliyāṇamittiraṉ muṉivaṉ ciṟappeṭut turaittal
   veṟuttuṭaṉ viṭuttaraci ṉaittuka ḷeṉappēr
       aṟapperu malaippoṟai yeṭuttava ṉaṭikkaṇ
       ciṟappiṉai yiyaṟṟalai* ciṉatteri maṉattāṉ
       maṟappaṭai yeṭuppateveṉ+ mālaimaṟa vēlōy.         268
       --------
       (pāṭam) * yiyaṟṟilai +yeṭuppatu meṉ.

   urai:- mālai maṟavēlōy- mālai yaṇinta maṟam poruntiya vēlai ēntiya
   vēntē, araciṉai - araca pōkattai, tukaḷ eṉa veṟuttu- aṟpap poruḷākak
   karutip paṟṟaṟaveṟuttu, uṭaṉ viṭuttu- avveṟupput tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉē
   oḻittuviṭṭu, pēr aṟapperu malaippoṟai yeṭuttavaṉ - tuṟavaṟamākiya
   periya malaiyai eṭuttut tāṅkiyirukkiṉṟa muṉivaṉuṭaiya tiruvaṭik kaṇṇē,
   ciṟappiṉai iyaṟṟalai- ciṟappākiya vaḻipāṭṭaic ceyyāmal, ciṉattu eri
   maṉattāl- ciṉattāl vetumpukiṉṟa maṉattōṭu, maṟappaṭai- vāṭpaṭaiyai,
   eṭuppatu evaṉ - kaiyil eṭuppatu eṟṟukku, uraippāyāka e-ṟu.
   vēlukku mālai yaṇivatum marapātaliṉ, "mālai maṟa vēlōy" eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭṭatu; itaṉai, "ivvē (ivvēṟ paṭaikaḷ). pīliyaṇintu mālaicūṭṭik
   kaṇṭiraṇōṉ kāḻtirutti neyyaṇintu kaṭiyuṭai viyaṇakaravvē"1 eṉpataṉālu
   maṟika. viḻumitu terinta viḻumiyōrkku, "noytā lamma tāṉē maiyaṟṟu
   vicumpuṟa vōṅkiya veṇkuṭai, muracu keḻu vēntar aracu keḻu tiruvē"2 eṉṟu
   cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   ------
       1.puṟa. 95. 2.puṟam. 75.

   tuṟavaṟattiṉ perumaiyum poṟuttaṟ karumaiyum tōṉṟa, "pēraṟapperumalai"
   yeṉṟum, ataṉai mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukum āṇmai nalam viḷaṅkap "poṟai yeṭuttavaṉ"
   eṉṟum, at takaiyōraik kaṇṭaviṭattu aracarāyiṉum vaḻipaṭuvatē uṭaṉ
   ceyaṟpāla teṉpāṉ, "ciṟappiṉai yiyaṟṟalai" yeṉṟum, nī ceyvatu tītu
   payappatām eṉṟa kuṟippāl, "maṟappaṭai yeṭuppa tevaṉ" eṉṟum vaṇikaṉ
   kūṟiṉāṉ.
   ākaveṉiṉ ākumivar aḻikaveṉiṉ aḻiyum*
       mēkamivaṇ varukaveṉiṉ varumatuvum vitiyiṉ
       ēkamaṉa rāmmuṉivar perumaiyitu vākum
       mākamaḻai vaṇkaimata yāṉai maṇimārpōy+         269
       --------
       (pāṭam) *ṉākavivaraḻiyaveṉilaḻika +muṭiyōy.

   urai:- māka maḻai vaṇkai- vāṉattil ulāvum maḻai mēkam pōlum vaḷḷaṉmai
   poruntiya kaiyiṉaiyum, matayāṉai- matam poruntiya yāṉaikaḷaiyum, maṇi
   mārpōy- maṇiyiḻaitta mārpaṇiyu muṭaiya vēntē, ivar- immuṉivar, āka eṉiṉ
   ākum- oruporuḷ ākkam peṟuka eṉpārāyiṉ avvāṟē ciṟitum piḻaiyiṉṟi
   ākkameytum, aḻika eṉiṉ aḻiyum- kēṭeytuka eṉṟāl avvāṟē akkaṇamē apporuḷ
   keṭum, ivaṇ mēkam varuka eṉiṉ - ippoḻutu ivviṭattē maḻai peyka eṉṟāl,
   vitiyiṉ - avar vitikkumāṟē, atuvum varum - ammēkamum vantu maḻai
   poḻiyum, ēkam maṉarām muṉivar perumai ituvākum - oru neṟiyil piṟaḻviṉṟi
   niṟkum maṉamuṭaiyarākiya im muṉivaratu perumai it taṉmaittākum. itaṉai
   nī niṉaiyā toḻukuvatu kūṭātu e-ṟu.
   itaṉai nī eṉpatu mutaliyaṉa kuṟippeccam. "yāṉaiyuṭaiya paṭai kāṇṭal
   iṉi" teṉpavākaliṉ, nāṟpaṭaiyiṉum mata yāṉaippaṭaiyai vitantōtiṉāṉ.
   maḻai vaṇkai poruṭ perumaiyum, matayāṉai paṭaipperumaiyum cuṭṭi
   niṉṟaṉa. ip perumaikaḷ ātaṟkum aḻitaṟku muriyavāvaṉa; avvākka vaḻivukaḷ
   iraṇṭaṉaiyum niṉaitta poḻutē ceyyavallareṉpāṉ, "ākaveṉiṉ....varumatuvum
   vitiyiṉ" eṉṟāṉ. poṟivaḻi yōṭippaḵṟalaip paṭṭalaitaliṉṟi oru neṟiyē
   niṟkum ciṟappuk kuṟittu, "ēkamaṉarāmiṉivar" eṉṟāṉ. muṉivar perumai itu
   veṉavē, aracar perumai poruḷ paṭai yeṉṟa iraṇṭāy
   vakutturaikkappaṭṭṭatu. aracarkkuriya mūvakaiyāṟṟaluḷ, perumai, poruḷ
   paṭai eṉṟa iraṇṭiṉmēl niṟkumeṉpatu araciyal neṟi.
   ----------
   aṭaintavarkaḷ kātaliṉo ṭamararara cāvar*
       kaṭantavarkaḷ tamatikaḻviṉ kaṭainarakiṉ vīḻvar
       aṭaintaniḻal pōlaruḷu muṉivumila raṭikaḷ
       kaṭantativa ṇulakiyalpu kaṭavuḷar ceyalē.         270
       -------
       (pāṭam) * kātali ṉamararaca rāvar

   urai :- aṭikaḷ - aracē, kātaliṉoṭu aṭaintavarkaḷ - uḷḷaṉpuṭaṉ immuṉivar
   tiruvaṭi yaṭaintavarkaḷ, amarar aracu āvar - tēvarkaracarākiya
   intirarākuvar, tamatu ikaḻviṉ kaṭantavar - tamatu mayakkavuṇarvāl
   aṟattiṉ varampu kaṭanta ceyalai ivarpāṟ ceypavar, kaṭai narakiṉ vīḻvar
   - ellā narakiṉum kīḻppaṭṭa narakattai yaṭaivar, aṭainta niḻalpōl -
   taṉṉai yaṭaintavarkkut taṭpam payantu vikāramiṉṟiyirukkum niḻalpōla,
   aruḷum muṉivum ilar - yāvarpālum viruppum veṟuppum ilarāvar, kaṭantatu
   ivaṇ ulakiyalpu - ivarkaḷ tuṟantatu immaikkaṇ ivvulaka pōkattiṟ cellum
   viruppēyākum, kaṭavuḷar ceyal – mēṟkoṇṭatu muṉivarkkuriya oṉṟaṉai
   vēṇṭalum vēṇṭāmaiyum illāta ceyalē. e - ṟu.
   tammai yaṭaintārkku aṟamuṇartti avarai naṉṉeṟik kaṇ uyttaliṉ, avar
   anneṟiyil amarar paravum ciṟappeytu vatu kuṟittu,
   "aṭaintavarkaḷ....amarararacāvar" eṉṟum ivarpāl mikka ceyal ceytu
   piḻaippōr narakil vīḻvatu kūṟi, aracaṉuḷḷattai neṟippaṭuttak karuti
   "kaṭantavarkaḷ....narakiṉ vīḻvar" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ, kaṭattal, muṉṉatu
   aṟavarampu kaṭantu mikkaceyal ceytal; piṉṉatu tuṟattal. ikaḻtaṟkuk
   kāraṇamākiya cerukkum aṟiyāmaiyum mayakkuṇarvum ellāmeyta ikaḻviṉ eṉak
   kāriyattāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. aṭaintār amarararacātaṟkum, kaṭantār narakil
   vīḻtaṟkum kāraṇarātaliṉ, ivar viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ uḷapōlum eṉṉum
   aiyamaṟuttaṟku, "aruḷummuṉivum ilar" eṉṟum, avai ulakiyalāyp
   piṟavikkētuvātal paṟṟit tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpāṉ. "kaṭantativaṇ
   ulakiyalpu" eṉṟum, mēṟkoṇṭatu viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa maṉappāṉmai yeṉṟaṟkuk
   "kaṭavuḷavar ceyalē" yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. vēṇṭutalum vēṇṭāmaiyum iṉmai
   kaṭavuṭṭaṉmaiyātaliṉ, "kaṭavuḷavar ceya"lāyiṟṟu.
   --------
   intirarkaḷ vantaṭipa ṇintaruḷu keṉiṉum
       cintaiyilar ventuyarka ḷeṇṇilakaḷ ceyiṉum*
       tattamviṉai yeṉṟunamar piṟareṉavu niṉaiyār
       antarami kantaruḷta vattaracar tārōy.         271
       ---------
       (pāṭam.) * cintaiyilar ventuyarkaṇiṉṉaṉarkaḷ ceyiṉum.

   urai:-- tārōy - mālai yaṇinta aracē. intirarkaḷ vantu - tēvarulaka
   vēntarkaḷākiya intirar tāmē pōntu, aṭipaṇintu - tam tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu
   vaṇaṅki, aruḷuka eṉiṉum - aruḷ ceyka eṉṟu vēṇṭip paraviṉālum,
   cintaiyilar - tam ceyaliṉ viḷaivaiyōrāmal, ventuyarkaḷ eṇṇilakaḷ
   ceyiṉum - koṭiya tīviṉaikaḷ aḷaviṟantaṉa ceytālum, tantam viṉaiyeṉṟum -
   iruvakaiyālum varum iṉpattuṉpaṅkaṭkuk kāraṇam tāmtām ceyta viṉaiyē eṉṟu
   niṉaittum, namar piṟar eṉavum - iṉpam ceytārai namakkuriyār eṉṟu
   uvattalum tuṉpañceytāraip pakaivareṉa veṟuttalum, niṉaiyār -
   niṉaiyāmalum, antaram ikantu - viṇṇulakattē peṟum iṉpattai veṟuttu,
   aruḷ tavattaracar - aruḷum tavamum nalkum ñāṉaviṉpattai nukarntoḻukum
   tavavēntarāvar e - ṟu.
   mēlē "aruḷum muṉivumilar" eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉai viḷakkumukattāl intirar
   parāvalum, ēṉaiyōr tīmaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi avviruviṉaikaḷaiyum immuṉivar
   karutum tiṟam itu veṉpāṉ "tantam viṉaiyeṉṟu" niṉaippar eṉṟum, "namar
   piṟar eṉa niṉaiyār" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. itu "ceñcān teṟiyiṉum cettiṉum
   pōḻiṉum neñ cōrntu nāṭā nilaimai" eṉpatu. cintittavaḻit tām ceyyum
   ceyaliṉ viḷaivu pulaṉāmātaliṉ, "cintaiyilar" eṉṟāṉ. antaram, ākupeyar.
   tavattukku aruḷ uruvātaliṉ, "aruḷ tavattaracar" eṉpa. ivar
   "antaramikantaruḷ tavattaracar" eṉṟatu tammaṭi paṇivār peṟum
   avviṇṇiṉpattait tām poruḷāka matiyāmai yuṇaraniṉṟatu.
   muṉivaṉaip paṇika eṉa vaṇikaṉ kūṟal
   ivvalaki ṉevvuyiru memmuyiri ṉēreṉ
       ṟavviya makaṉṟaruḷcu rantuyir vaḷarkkum
       vimaiyiṉ niṉṟavarti runtaṭi paṭintuṉ
       viṉaikka ṭantaṉaivi ḷaṅku*viṟal vēlōy.         272
       ----------
       (pāṭam.) * kaṭantuyir viḷaṅkum

   urai:-viṟal vēlōy - viṟal poruntiya vēlaiyuṭaiyāy, ivvulakil - iv
   vulakattil, evvuyirum em uyiriṉ nēr eṉṟu - ettakaiyavuyirum emmuṭaiya
   uyiraiyē nikarkkum eṉṟu karuti, avviyam akaṉṟu - avaṟṟai puṟakkaṇikkum
   mayakkamiṉṟi, aruḷ curantu - aruḷ purintu, uyir vaḷarkkum cevvimaiyiṉ -
   avvuyirkaḷaip purakkum cemmai neṟiyiṉkaṇ, niṉṟavar - niṟkiṉṟa
   immēlōruṭaiya, tiruntaṭi paṭintu - aḻakiya caraṇaṅkaḷilē mūḻki, uṉ
   vevviṉai kaṭantaṉai - uṉṉuṭaiya tīviṉaiyākiya aḻukkiṉaip pōkki, viḷaṅku
   - cīvacuttiyiṉai eytuvāyāka e-ṟu.
   virintatu tokuttal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi, mēlē viriyak kūṟivantavaṟṟait
   tokuttu, maṉṉuyirait taṉṉuyireṉak karutalum, avaṟṟiṟku uṟukaṇ ceyyātu
   aruḷ purivatum, avaṟṟiṟku aṟamuṇarttic cemmai neṟikkaṇ
   niṉṟoḻukuvittalum immuṉivarar kaṭaṉ eṉṟāṉāyiṟṟu. ittakaiya periyōr
   tiruvaṭiyil maṉamoṉṟi vaḻipāṭāṟṟiyavaḻi niṉaikkum av viyalpē
   yuṇṭāmeṉṟum, atuvē vāyilāka nī uyirkkolai tavirntu avaṟṟai vaḷarkkum
   uyar ceyalai mēṟkoṇṭu, pēriṉpattukkuriya uyirttūymai (cīvacutti)
   eytuvāy eṉṟum moḻivāṉ, "tiruntaṭi paṭintu uṉ vevviṉai kaṭantaṉai
   viḷaṅku" eṉṟāṉ. tiruvaṭi aruṭkaṭalātaliṉ, ataṉai vaṇaṅkutalaip
   paṭitaleṉṟāṉ. piṟāṇṭum "veṉṟavar caraṇamūḻki" eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. viṉaimācu
   nīṅkiyavaḻi uyir tūymai peṟuvatu kuṟittu, "kaṭantaṉai viḷaṅku" eṉṟāṉ.
   kaṭantaṉai, muṟṟeccam.
   maṉṉaṉ muṉivaṉai yāvaṉeṉa aṟital
   eṉṟiṉitu kūṟumvaṇi kaṉcolika ḻātē
       kaṉṟuciṉa muṅkarata lappaṭaiyu māṟṟi
       iṉṟivaṉai yeṉṉaitoḻu māṟaḷiya ṉiyāvaṉ
       kaṉṟutukaḷ tuṉṟukaru mēṉiyiṉa ṉeṉṟāṉ.         273

   urai:-- eṉṟu - eṉapatāka. vaṇikaṉ iṉitu kūṟum col -
   kaliyāṇamittiraṉākiya vaṇikaṉ iṉitākak kūṟiya collai, ikaḻātu -
   nekiḻātu mēṟkoṇṭu, kaṉṟu ciṉamum - mikkuniṉṟa vekuḷiyiṉaiyum,
   karatalappaṭaiyum - kaiyil uruvippiṭittirunta vāṭpaṭaiyiṉaiyum, māṟṟi -
   muṟaiyē taṇittum uṟaiyiṟ cerukiyum, iṉṟu ivaṉai toḻumāṟu eṉṉai - ippōtu
   ivaṉait toḻum vakaiyātu, kaṉṟutukaḷ tuṉṟu karumēṉiyiṉaṉ - mikka puḻuti
   paṭintu karuttamēṉiyiṉai yuṭaiyaṉākiya, aḷiyaṉ - aḷikkattakka ivaṉ,
   yāvaṉ - yāvaṉākum, eṉṟāṉ – eṉṟu yacōmati avvaṇikaṉai viṉaviṉāṉ e - ṟu.
   kaṉṟu ciṉamum karatalattiṟ paṭaiyumēntit tītu kuṟitta cintaiyaṉāyirunta
   vēntaṉ maṉattil aṉpum muṉivaṉpāl naṉmatippum uṇṭāmāṟu pēciyatu kuṟittu,
   "iṉitu kūṟum vaṇikaṉcol" eṉṟum, muṉivaṉpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭaṟkuriya nallūḻ
   piṉṉēyiruntu ūkkutalāl, "ikaḻātu" eṉṟum, kūṟiṉār. ciṉattai
   māṟṟutalāvatu taṇivittal; vāṭpaṭaiyai māṟṟutal, maṟuvalum uṟaiyiṟ
   cerukikkōṭal. cutatta muṉivaṉatu mēṉiyiṉaik kaṇṭu aruvaruttu niṉṟamai
   tōṉṟa, "kaṉṟu tukaḷ tuṉṟu karumēṉiyaṉ" eṉṟum, "aḷiyaṉ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
   mēṉiyiṉ tōṟṟattāl toḻutaṟkut taṉ uḷḷam cellāmaiyaik kuṟippāṟ
   kāṭṭuvāṉāy, "iṉṟivaṉai eṉṉai toḻumāṟu" eṉkiṉṟāṉ. kaṉṟal, mikutal.
   vaṇikaṉ muṉivaṉatu varalāṟu kūṟal
   iṅkalaku toḻumuṉiyai yāveṉeṉi ṉitukēḷ
       kaṅkaikula tilakaṉivaṉ kaliṅkapati yataṉaip
       poṅkupuya valiyilpotu viṉṟimuḻu tāṇṭa
       ciṅkamiva ṉeṉṟuteḷi tēntuṇariṉ vēntē.*         274
       ------------
       (pāṭam.) * tērntuṇar viṉ vēntē; tērntuṇariṉ vēntē.

   urai:-tēntuṇariṉ vēntē - tēṉ poruntiya pūṅ kottukkaḷāliyaṉṟa mālai
   yaṇinta aracacē, iṅkē - iṅkē eḻuntaruḷi yirukkiṉṟa, ulaku toḻu muṉiyai
   - aṟivuṭaiyōr paravum ic cutattamuṉivaṉai, yāvaṉ eṉiṉ - yāvaṉ eṉṟu
   kēṭakiṉṟāyāyiṉ, itukēḷ - yāṉ kūṟappōku mitaṉaik kēṭpāyāka. ivaṉ kaṅkai
   kulatilakaṉ - ivaṉ kaṅkai kulattiṟpiṟanta mēṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉ, kaliṅkapati -
   kaliṅka nāṭṭiṟku aracaṉ, ataṉai - ak kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉai, poṅku
   puyavaliyil - uyarnta taṉ tōḷ vaṉmaiyāl, ivaṉ potuviṉṟi muḻutu āṇṭa
   ciṅkam - ivaṉ potuccoṟ poṟātu nāṭu muḻutum tāṉē yāṭci purinta
   ciṅkampōlum āṟṟaluṭaiyaṉāvāṉ, eṉṟu teḷi - eṉṟu teḷivāy aṟivāyāka e-ṟu.
   tuṇar pūṅkottu; ākupeyar. ulaku, uyarntōr mēṟṟātaliṉ, uyarntōrāl
   toḻappaṭum muṉivaṉai, "ulaku toḻu muṉiyai" eṉṟāṉ. tilakaṉ, mēlāṉavaṉ.
   nilavulaku aracarellārkkum potu veṉṉuñ collaik kēṭkappoṟā tuvaliyuṭaiya
   taṉakkē uriyatu eṉṉum ūkkamum uṭkōḷum uṭaiyaṉātal tōṉṟa, "potu viṉṟi
   muḻutāṇṭa" eṉṟum, pakaiyaracarkku accattai viḷaittalāl "ciṅkam" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāṉ. mēṉiyiṉ niṟamum tavakkōlamum piṟavum kaṇṭu kaṉṟiya ciṉamum
   kaiyil vāṭpaṭaiyum ēnti maṉam maruṇṭu niṉṟa yacōtamatikku
   aṟivuṟattukiṉṟā ṉātaliṉ, "ciṅkamivaṉ eṉṟu teḷi" eṉṟu vaṇikaṉ ceppiṉāṉ.
   aracaṉpāl viyappu meyppaṭavē, kaṇṭa vaṇikaṉ mēlum cila kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
   mēkameṉa miṉṉumeṉa * villumeṉa vallē
       pōkamoṭu poruḷiḷamai poṉṟunaṉi yeṉṟē
       ākatuṟa varuḷperuku maṟeṉeṭata ṉiyalē +
       pōkamiku poṉṉulaku pukuvaṉeṉa niṉaivāṉ.         275
       ------------
       (pāṭam.) * miṉṉiṉeṭu. + ṉayalē.

   urai:-mēkameṉa miṉṉumeṉa villumeṉa - mēkamum miṉṉum intiravil lumpōla,
   pōkamoṭu poruḷ iḷamai - muṟaiyē pōkamum poruḷum iḷamaiyum, vallē naṉi
   poṉṟum eṉṟu. viraiya mikakkeṭum eṉṟu, āka - teḷiya vuṇarntāṉāka,
   tuṟavu-tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu, aruḷ perukum aṟaṉōṭu - aruḷ mikutaṟkuriya
   nallaṟattōṭu, ataṉ iyal - ataṉāl iyalum payaṉākiya, pōka miku poṉṉulaku
   - iṉpam mikuvikkum tuṟakka vulakai, pukuvaṉ eṉa niṉaivāṉ-aṭaivēṉ eṉṟu
   akkaliṅkapati niṉaivāṉāyiṉaṉ e-ṟu.
   mēkam mutaliya mūṉṟum muṟaiyē pōkam mutiliya mūṉṟaṟkum uvamai. āmiṭattu
   viraiyavākātu meṉamelac ciṟitu ciṟitāy ātalum keṭumiṭattu mika viraivil
   iṟappavum mikutiyākak keṭutalum ippōkam mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum iyalpātaliṉ,
   "vallē naṉi poṉṟum" eṉṟu kaṇṭāṉ. avvāṟu kaṇṭāṟkut tuṟavē tuṇiporuḷāyt
   tōṉṟiya teṉpār, "eṉṟēyāka" eṉṟum "tuṟavu niṉaivāṉ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   aruḷai vaḷarkkum nallaṟam caiṉa tarumameṉ ṟuṇarntu ataṉaik
   kaṭaippiṭittamai tōṉṟa, "aruḷ peruku maṟaṉeṭu" eṉṟum, ataṉai
   mēṟkoṇṭoḻukiyavaḻi yeytum payaṉ tuṟakkaviṉpam eṉṟaṟku, "ataṉ iyalē
   pōkamiku poṉṉulaku" eṉṟum niṉaittāṉ. iyalum payaṉai iyaleṉṟē yoḻintār;
   vaḻakkiṉ payaṉai "aṉpuṟ ṟamarnta vaḻakkeṉpa" 1 eṉṟāṟpōla. poṉṉulaku
   niṉaitaṟ kētu īteṉpār, "pōkamiku poṉṉulaku" eṉṟāreṉka.
   -------------
       1. kuṟaḷ. 75. (pāṭam.) * yaṇitēr.

   nāṭunaka raṅkaḷu nalaṅkoḷmaṭa vārum
       āṭukoṭi yāṉai atir tēr* puravi kālāḷ
       cūṭumuṭi mālaikuḻai tōḷvaḷaiyo ṭāram
       āṭaimuta lāyiṉavo ṭakalkaeṉa viṭṭāṉ.         276

   urai:-- nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum - nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷaiyum, nalam koḷ maṭavārum -
   aḻakukoṇṭa makaḷirkaḷaiyum, āṭukoṭi yāṉaiyoṭu - acaikiṉṟa koṭiyēntiya
   yāṉaip paṭaiyiṉaiyum, atir - pakaivarkku naṭukkattaicceyyum, tēr puravi
   kālāḷoṭu - tērppaṭai kutiraippaṭai kālāṭpaṭaikaḷaiyum, cūṭu muṭi
   mālaiyoṭu - tāṉ aṇikiṉṟa muṭiyaiyum mālaiyaiyum, kuḻai toḷvaḷaiyoṭu
   āram - kātilaṇiyum kuḻaiyiṉaiyum tōḷilaṇiyum vaḷaikaḷaiyum mārpilaṇiyum
   muttumālaiyiṉaiyum, āṭai mutalāyiṉavoṭu - paṭṭiṉum paruttiyiṉumiyaṉṟa
   uyariya uṭai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, akalka eṉa viṭṭāṉ – nīkkumiṉ eṉat
   tuṟantoḻittāṉ e-ṟu.
   nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum eṉpatai ummaittokaiyākkātu viṉaittokaiyākki
   ulakamuḻutum tiraṇṭu oruṅkuvariṉum vaḻaṅkat tavā vaḷamuṭaimaiyiṉ ēṉai
   nāṭṭavarum nakarattavarum virumpum nakaraṅkaḷum eṉṟuraittalumoṉṟu.
   nalam, iṉpa nukarccikku vēṇṭum peṇmai nalam. maṭavār, iḷaiyamakaḷir.
   tōḷvaḷai, vākuvalayam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. oṭuvum ummaiyum eṇṇup poruṭṭu.
   oṭuveṉpatu ēṉaiyiṭattum kūṭṭappaṭṭatu, "eṉṟum eṉavum oṭuvum tōṉṟi,
   oṉṟuvaḻi yuṭaiya eṇṇiṉuṭ pirintē" eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. tuṟavuḷḷam muṟuki
   niṟṟaliṉ, mālai mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇarntārai vēṇṭāveṉa vilakkiṉāṉ eṉka.
   viṭutal, paṟṟu viṭutal, mutalāyiṉa eṉpuḻi eytutaṟkuriya cāriyai
   vikārattāl nilaiyātāyiṟṟu.
   ---------
   vāṉavaru maṇṇiṉmicai yaracarkaḷu malaimēl
       tāṉavarum vantutoḻu tavavuruvu koṇṭāṉ
       ūṉamaṉa miṉṟiyuyir kaṭkuṟuti yuṉṉik*
       kāṉamalai nāṭukaḷka lantutiri tiṉṟāṉ.         277
       ----------
       (pāṭam) * yuḷḷi.

   urai:-- vāṉavarum - viṇṇulakattut tēvarkaḷum, maṇṇiṉ micai yaracarkaḷum
   - maṇṇulakattu vēntarkaḷum malaimēl tāṉavarum-cēṭi mutaliya
   malaikaḷilvāḻum vittiyātararkaḷum, vantu-taṉpāl vantu, toḻutavavuruvu
   koṇṭāṉ-toḻutu vaṇaṅkum tavavoḻukkattai mēṟkoṇṭa iccutattaṉ, maṉam ūṉam
   iṉṟi- maṉattiṭattē kuṟṟamāṉa niṉaivu ciṟitu miṉṟi, uyirkaṭku uṟuti
   yuṉṉi-uyirkaṭku uṟutiyāṉavaṟṟaiyē niṉaintu, kāṉam malai nāṭukaḷ-kāṭum
   malaiyum nāṭum mutaliya ellā viṭaṅkaṭkum ceṉṟu, kalantu tirikiṉṟāṉ-
   ōriṭattum nilaipēriṉṟit tirintoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   cutattaṉ mēṟkoṇṭirunta tavakkōlattiṉ ciṟappum oḻukkattiṉ viḻuppamum
   kūṟuvāṉ, vāṉavarum tāṉavarum maṇṇiṭattu vēntarum vantu toḻum ciṟappiṉai
   yeṭuttukkūṟiṉāṉ. naṉṉiṉaivē niṉaiyum maṉamuṭaiyaṉāyiṉum, paṇṭaip
   payiṟci vācaṉaiyāl neñcil niṉaivuvāyilākak kuṟṟam ciṟitu muṇṭākāvāṟu
   taṉṉaik kāttukkoṇ ṭoḻukumāṟu tōṉṟa, "ūṉam maṉam iṉṟi uyirkaṭ kuṟuti
   yuṉṉi" eṉṟum, kāṉam malai nāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ ētēṉumōriṭattē
   nilaittiruppiṉ atuvāyilāka viruppu veṟuppukkaḷ piṟantuviṭu mātaliṉ,
   "kāṉa malai nāṭukaḷ kalantu tirikiṉṟāṉ" eṉṟu vaṇikaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
   kalanteṉavē, ōriṭattum taṅkāmai peṟṟām.

   yāṉumala teṉatumala titamumala teṉṉum*
       māṉamuṭai mātavaṉiṉ mēṉimaki ḻāṉāy
       ēṉaiviṉai mācutaṉa turuviṉiṟu vātē†
       ñāṉavoḷi nakaiceykuṇam nāḷumaṇi kiṉṟāṉ.         278
       --------------
       (pāṭam) *ṉiyalātē.

   urai:- iṉmēṉi-taṉakku iṉitāy amainta mēṉiyāṉatu, yāṉu malatu eṉatu
   malatu- yāṉum aṉṟu eṉakkēyuriyatu maṉṟu, itamum alatu-nalam ceyvatu
   maṉṟu, eṉṉum-eṉṟu karutum, māṉamuṭai mātavaṉ- aṟivupperumaiyiṉai
   yuṭaiya mātavaṉ, makiḻāṉāy-ataṉpāl paṟṟuc ciṟitum ilaṉāy, taṉatu
   uruviṉ-taṉatu mēṉiyil, ēṉaiviṉaimācu-ēṉaiviṉaikaḷākiya kuṟṟattāluṇṭākum
   vacaiyiṉai, niṟuvātu-mēṟkoḷḷātu, ñāṉavoḷi nakai ceykuṇam-ñāṉavoḷi
   viḷaṅkac ceyyum naṟkuṇattāluṇṭākum icaiyākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai, nāḷum
   aṇikiṉṟāṉ-eppōtum mēṟkoṇṭirup pāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   "uruviṉ mikkatōr uṭampatu peṟutalumaritē"1 eṉpa vākaliṉ, "iṉmēṉi"
   yeṉṟār. uṭaṟkuṇṭākum ākkak kēṭukaḷāl uyirkku iṉpamum tuṉpamum
   uṇṭātaliṉ, "eṉatu" eṉṟum, uyir nīṅkuṅkāṟum uṭaṉiruntu piṉpu piritaliṉ
   "eṉatu" eṉṟum, uṭalōṭu kūṭiyirukkumaḷavum uyirait taṉṉaip
   piriyavoṇṇātapaṭi paṟṟucceyvittaliṉ, "itam" eṉṟum, uṭampu makkaḷāṟ
   karutappaṭiṉum akkaruttaṉaittum piṟappiṟ kētuvāyt tuṉpam payattalpaṟṟi
   "yāṉumalatu eṉata malatu itamum alatu eṉṉum māṉamuṭai mātavaṉ" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāṉ. uṭalai nilaiyil poruḷeṉṟaṟintu, atukoṇṭu nilaitta payaṉaic
   ceytu koḷḷutaliṉ perumai piṟitillai yātaliṉ, "māṉamuṭaiyamātavaṉ"
   eṉṟār. ēṉait tīviṉai muṟṟum paḻiyum tīrāvacaiyum payattaliṉ,
   "viṉaimācu" eṉṟum, nalviṉai pukaḻum ñāṉamum payattaliṉ, ataṉai aḻaku
   tarum aṇiyākki, "nāḷum aṇikiṉṟāṉ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. vacai eṉṟum nilait
   tiruppatu paṟṟi "niṟuvātu" eṉṟār.

       1 cīvaka. 2752

   īṭiṉmuṉi yōkiṉatu perumai yiḵtiṟaiva*
       kāṭupaṭu kolaiyiṉoṭu kaṭiyaviṉai niṉṉaik
       kūṭuvato ḻintatukoḷ† iṉṟukolai vēlōy
       nāṭuvateṉ ñamali yivai naṇukalakaṇ kāṇāy‡         279
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *yeṉiliṟaiva † toḻintatu kol
       ‡ yivaṉalluṟavu kāṇāy; yivai naṇukalarkaḷ kāṇāy.

   urai:- iṟaiva-aracē, īṭuil muṉi-oppaṟṟa iccutatta muṉivaṉatu, yōkiṉatu
   perumai-yōkattiṉ perumai, iḵtu-ittaṉmaiyākum, kolaivēlōy-
   uyirkaḷaikkollum vēlaiyuṭaiya vēntē, kāṭupaṭu kolaiyiṉoṭu-kāṭṭiṭattē
   ceyta uyirkkolaiyāl uṇṭākiya, kaṭiya viṉai-tuṉpam tarum tīviṉaiyāṉatu,
   iṉṟu kūṭuvatoḻintatu koḷ-ippōtu nī immuṉivaṉaik kaṇṭataṉāl niṉṉai
   vantaṭaiyātu oḻintateṉṟu koḷvāyāka, nāṭuvatu eṉ-iṉi vēṟu pala niṉaivatu
   vēṇṭā, ñamali ivai naṇukala-nī ēviya nāykaḷ im muṉivaṉai naṇṇāvāyiṉa,
   kaṇkāṇ-avaṟṟai niṉ kaṇkaḷālē naṉku kāṇpāyāka e-ṟu
   immuṉivaṉpāl yacōmatiyai āṟṟuppaṭuttu avaṉ kūṟum aṟattāl
   naṉṉeṟippaṭuttum karuttiṉa ṉātaliṉ, kali yāṇa mittiraṉ "īṭiṉmuṉi
   yōkiṉatu perumai iḵtu" eṉṟu muṉmoḻintu koṇṭu, avaṉaik kaṇṭa kāṭciyāl
   varum payaṉ itu veṉpāṉ "kāṭupaṭu kolaiyiṉoṭu kaṭiya viṉai niṉṉaik
   kūṭuva toḻintatu koḷ" eṉṟāṉ. oṭu, āṉurupiṉ poruṭṭu. taṉ kūṟṟiṉai
   yacōmati iṉitu ēṟṟuk kōṭaṟku, yāṉ kūṟuvatait tuṇitaṟkuc cāṉṟu vēṟu
   virumpa vēṇṭā eṉpāṉ "nāṭuvateṉ" eṉṟum, tīṅku ceyyap pōnta nāykkūṭṭam
   atuceyya naṇukamāṭṭātu cēymaikkaṇ niṉṟoḻintamaiyē pōtiya cāṉṟām eṉṟum
   ataṉai nī niṉkaṇ ṇetirē kāṇkiṉṟāy eṉṟum kūṟuvāṉ "ñamaliyivai naṇukala"
   eṉṟum "kaṇkāṇāy" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ, kaṇkāṇ eṉṟatu kāṭciyaḷavai kāṭṭiyavāṟu,
   yacōmati muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyil taṉ talaiyaiyarintu paliyiṭak karututal
   eṉṟava ṉuḷaṅkoḷa viyampiṉa ṉiyampac*
       ceṉṟutiru vaṭimalarkaḷ ceṉṉimicai yaṇiyā
       iṉṟeṉatu piḻaitaṇiya eṉṟalai yarintē
       niṉṟamuṉi caraṇiṭuva‡ leṉṟuniṉai kiṉṟāṉ.         280
       ---------------
       (pāṭam) *viyappa. † caraṇiṉiṭa.

   urai:- eṉṟu-eṉpatāka, avaṉ uḷam koḷa- yacōmatiyiṉ maṉam ēṟkumāṟu,
   iyampiṉaṉ-colliya kaliyāṇa mittiraṉ, iyampa-muṉivaṉai vaṇaṅkumāṟu
   uraikka, ceṉṟu-arukē ceṉṟu, tiruvaṭimalarkaḷ ceṉṉimicai
   yaṇiyā-muṉivaṉuṭaiya aḻakiya pātamākiya tāmaraippūvai vaṇaṅkit taṉ
   talaiyil cūṭikkoṇṭu, iṉṟu-ippōtu, eṉatu piḻai taṇiya-eṉ kuṟṟamel lām
   nīṅka, eṉtalai arintu- eṉṉuṭaiya talaiyai yaṟuttu,
   niṉṟamuṉi-tavaneṟiyil caliyātu niṉṟa cutatta muṉivaṉuṭaiya caraṇ
   iṭuval-tiruvaṭiyil vaippēṉ, eṉṟu niṉaikiṉṟāṉ-eṉṟu yacōmati
   niṉaippāṉāyiṉaṉ. e-ṟu
   iyampiṉaṉ, viṉaimutalmē ṉiṉṟa viṉaippeyar. iyampak kēṭṭatum yacōmati
   muṉivaṉaṭikaḷai vaṇaṅka niṉaittaliṉ, vaṇaṅkumā ṟuraittāṉeṉṟu
   uraikkappaṭṭatu. tiruvaṭiyai malarāka uruvakam ceytamaiyiṉ,
   vaṇakkattaic ceṉṉimicai yaṇitaleṉṟār. uyirkkolaiyē ceytu kaṉṟiya
   uḷḷamuṭaiya maṟavēntaṉātaliṉ, talaiyarintu vaḻipaṭum maṟavar pōlavē
   niṉaippāṉ "eṉtalai yarintu niṉṟamuṉi caraṇiṭuval" eṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟāṉ.
   "niṉṟamuṉi" yeṉavē, tavayōkattil cutattaṉ calippiṉṟi niṟkumāṟu peṟṟām.
   ivvāṟu niṉaitta vēntaṉ taṉ uṭaivāḷai yuruvat toṭaṅkutaliṉ, avaṉ karuttu
   vaṇikaṉ naṉku aṟintukoḷḷattakka nilaiyil meyppaṭṭamai kaṇṭu, avaṉ mēlum
   cilakūṟi aracaṉ karuttai māṟṟukiṉṟāṉ; atuvarum ceyyuḷiṟ
   kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
   kaliyāṇamittiraṉ taṭutturaittal
   iṉṉatu niṉaintativa ṉiṉṟukai yeṭuttē
       maṉṉaniṉ maṉattatu viṭuntiṭu maṉattil
       taṉṉuyiriṉ maṉṉuyir vaḷarkkaitaka vāṉāl
       niṉṉuyirai nīkaḷaiyi ṉiṉṉaruḷa teṉṉām.         281

   urai:- ivaṉ niṉaintatu iṉṉatu-iv yacōmati niṉaittatu ittaṉmaittākum,
   eṉṟu-eṉṟu vaṇikaṉāṉa kaliyāṇamittiraṉ uṇarntu, kaiyeṭuttu-kaiyeṭuttut
   toḻutu taṭuttu, maṉṉa-vēntē, niṉmaṉattatu viṭuttiṭu-niṉmaṉatil niṉaitta
   eṇṇattai viṭṭoḻippāyāka, maṉṉuyir-ulakil nilaipeṟum uyirkaḷai, maṉattil
   taṉ uyiriṉ vaḷarkkai-maṉatil taṉṉuṭai uyir pōlak karutippēṇi
   vaḷarppatu, takavu-takutiyākum. āṉāl-avvāṟāyiṉ, nī niṉ uyiraik
   kaḷaiyiṉ-nī niṉṉuṭaiya uyiraip pōkkuvāyāyiṉ, iṉṉaruḷ atu eṉṉām-piṉpu nī
   ceytaṟkuriya iṉiya aruṭceykaiyāṉatu evvāṟu iyaluvatām e-ṟu
   yacōmati taṉ talaiyai yarintu muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyi liṭak karutiya
   karuttait teḷiya vuṇarntukoṇṭamaiyiṉ "iṉṉatu niṉainta tivaṉ" eṉṟum,
   acceyalait taṭuttaliṉāl "kaiyeṭuttu niṉ maṉattatu viṭuttiṭu" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāṉ. tāṉ ceyta kuṟṟamellām keṭavēṇṭiṉaṉ, aracaṉ aruḷaṟam mēṟkoḷvatē
   takkateṉkiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ "maṉattil taṉṉuyiriṉ maṉṉuyir vaḷarkkai takavu"
   eṉṟum, ataṉaic ceyyum viṉaimutal nī yātaliṉ, nī niṉṉuyiraip pōkkiviṭiṉ,
   aruḷviṉai yiṉṟāki, niṉkaruttum niṟaiveytā teṉpāṉ "iṉṉaruḷa teṉṉām"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāl niṉṉaruḷatu eṉṟu koṇṭu niṉakkuriya
   aruṭceykai iṉṟātaliṉ, ataṉāl orupayaṉum iṉṟām eṉṟu uraittukkoḷka.
   vaṇikaṉ mēlum cila kūṟal
   muṉṉamurai ceytaporuḷ muṭintilatu muṭiyap
       piṉṉumikai piṟavumuḷa* pēcutiṟa niṉaivun
       tuṉṉuvayiṉ† muṉṉitu tuṇintupiḻai tūrāy
       piṉṉuniṉai kiṉṟavitu pīḻaiperi teṉṟāṉ.         282
       -----------
       (pāṭam) *piṟavumurai. † tuṉṉuyiriṉ.

   urai:- muṉṉam uraiceyta poruḷ muṭintilatu.- muṉṉē yāṉ colliya poruḷ
   iṉṉum muṭiyavillai. muṭiya-collimuṭittaṟku, piṉṉum mikai piṟavum uḷa-
   colliya apporuṭkum mikutiyāka vēṟu palavum uḷḷaṉa, pēcutiṟam niṉaivum
   tuṉṉuvayiṉ muṉ-vāyāṟ pēcum collum maṉattāl niṉaiyum niṉaivum oṉṟucēru
   muṉṉē, itu tuṇintu-ivvuyirkkolaiyaic ceyyak karuti, piḻai
   tūrāy-paṇṭaippāvattaip pōkkāyāyiṉai, piṉṉum niṉaikiṉṟa itu-piṉṉarum nī
   niṉ talaiyaiyariyak karutum ikkaruttu, pīḻai peritu eṉṟāṉ-mikavum
   tuṉpam payakkum tīviṉaiyākum eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ e-ṟu muṉpu, muṉivaṉatu
   tavapperumai yītu eṉṟum, avaṉuṭaiya kāṭcicciṟappāl aracaṉuṭaiya tīviṉai
   nīṅkiṉa eṉṟum ataṟku avaṉēviya nāykaḷ muṉivaṉai naṇukāmai takka
   kāṭciyaḷavai yāmeṉṟum kūṟi iṉic ceyyattakuvaṉavum piṟavum kūṟimuṭikku
   muṉṉē aracaṟkuc cūḻcci piṟantatu meyppaṭṭamai kaṇṭu taṉ pēccu
   neṟimāṟiyatu kūṟuvāṉ, "muṉṉa murai...piṟavumuḷa" eṉṟum, ippēccu muṭivil
   aracaṉ tāṉ ceyyattakkataṉai vāyviṭṭuc collic ceyalvakai yārāyntu
   tuṇivatuvēṇṭu meṉṟaṟku, "pēcutiṟam niṉaivum tuṉṉuvaiyiṉ muṉ itu
   tuṇintu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. eṇṇummai vikārattāl tokkatu. ittuṇivu, mēlum
   uyirkkolaiyāyp paṇṭu cey tirunta pāvattai mikutippaṭuttutaliṉ,
   "piḻaitūrāy" eṉṟāṉ.pāvam uyiraik kīḻ nōkkit taḷḷutaliṉ, "tūrāy" eṉṟāṉ.
   tūrtal, aṭaittal. "piṉṉum niṉaikiṉṟa itu" eṉṟatu kūṟiyatukūṟa lāyiṉum,
   "peṟṟataṉ peyartturai niyamapporuṭ" ṭātalpaṟṟi, niyamittavāṟu. pīḻai,
   tuṉpam.
   maṉṉaṉ teḷintu muṉivaṉaip paravutal
   maṉṉavaṉ maṉattai viṭuttaruḷ* vaḷarkkuñ
       coṉṉavil cutattamuṉi tuṇaimala+ raṭikkaṇ
       ceṉṉimuṭi tuṉṉumalar ceṉṟuṟa vaṇaṅkip
       paṉṉiyaru viṟaivavemar pavamuḻutu meṉṟāṉ.         283
       ----------
       (pāṭam,) * virittaruḷ + toṉmalar, tuṇaimalar.

   urai:- maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya yacōmati, maṉattatai viṭuttu - taṉ
   maṉattil niṉaitta eṇṇattai viṭṭoḻittu, aruḷ vaḷarkkum col navil
   cutattamuṉi - aruḷākiya aṟattai mikuvikkum coṟkaḷaiyē collum
   iyaṟkaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya cutatta muṉivaṉuṭaiya, tuṇaimalar aṭikkaṇ -
   tāmaraipōlum iraṇṭu tiruvaṭiyilum, ceṉṉi muṭi tuṉṉumalar ceṉṟu uṟa
   vaṇaṅki - talaiyil cūṭiya muṭiyiṟkoṇṭa pūvāṉatu muṉivaṉ tiruvaṭiyiṟ
   ceṉṟu poruntumāṟu vīḻntu vaṇaṅki, iṟaiva - perumāṉē, emar pavam muḻutum
   - emmuṉṉō raṭainta piṟappu vakai muṟṟum, paṉṉiyaruḷ eṉṟāṉ-uraittaruḷka
   eṉṟu vēṇṭiniṉṟāṉ. e-ṟu.
   vaṇikaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟai uḷaṅkoḷak kēṭṭut teḷivey tiṉamaiyiṉ, "maṉattai
   viṭuttāṉ" eṉṟār. evvuyirkkum aruḷē niṉaitalum ceytalum uṭaiyaṉātaliṉ,
   muṉivaṉai, "aruḷ vaḷarkkum col navil cutatta muṉi" eṉṟār. col mēl vait
   tōtiṉārāyiṉum ēṉai niṉaivu ceyalkaḷum koḷḷappaṭum eṉka. taṉ taṉmaiyiṉṟi
   ammuṉivaṉ ceyyum aruḷ vayatta ṉāṉamai tōṉṟa
   "tuṇaimalaraṭikkaṇ........vaṇaṅki"ṉāṉ eṉa vuṇarka. aṟaṅkēṭkiṉṟamaiyiṉ,
   "iṟaiva" eṉṟum, taṉ muṉṉōr ceyta ceyviṉaippayaṉai yaṟital
   vēṭkaiyuṭaiyaṉātaliṉ "emar pavam muḻutum paṉṉiyaruḷ" eṉṟum vēṇṭiṉāṉ.
   paṉṉiyaruḷ, oru collāy uraikka eṉṉum poruḷ paṭa niṉṟatu. iḵtu uyarcol.
   muṉivaṉ acōkaṉ eytiya piṟappiṉaik kūṟutal
   āṅkumuṉi yavatiyi ṉaṟintaporu ḷataṉai
       vāṅkiyava ṉuṇarumvakai vaittaruḷcey kiṉṟāṉ
       īṅkumu ṉiyaṟṟiya tavattiṉi ṉacōkaṉ
       ōṅkupuka ḻamarulaka moṉṟiṉu ḷuvantāṉ.         284

   urai:-āṅku-appoḻutu, muṉi-cutatta muṉivaṉ, avatiyiṉ aṟinta poruḷataṉai
   vāṅki-avati ñāṉattālē aṟiyappaṭuvatāyirunta muṟpavapporuḷaiyuṇarntu,
   avaṉ uṇarum vakai vaittu-yacōmati naṉku uṇarntu koḷḷum muṟaiyil
   vakuttamaittu, aruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉ- uraippāṉāyiṉāṉ, īṅku-ivvulakattē, muṉ
   iyaṟṟiya tavattiṉiṉ-muṉṉē ceyta tavattiṉ payaṉāka, ōṅku pukaḻ
   amarulakam oṉṟiṉuḷ-mutaṟkaṇ uyarnta pukaḻaiyuṭaiya tēvarulakaṅkaḷ
   oṉṟiṉuḷḷē, acōkaṉ uvantāṉ-acōkaṉākiya maṉṉaṉ, iṉpuṟṟiruntāṉ e-ṟu.
   avati, cammiyañāṉavakai aintaṉuḷ oṉṟu. avai mati ñāṉam, cutañāṉam,
   avatiñāṉam, maṉappariyayañāṉam, kēvala ñāṉam eṉpaṉavākum. vikalam,
   tūlam, cakala niccayam eṉavarum vakai mūṉṟaṉuḷ, inta avati ñāṉam
   vikalattiṉ pāṟpaṭum eṉpar. inta ñāṉattāl iṟappil nikaḻntaṉavum, etirvil
   nikaḻvaṉavum aṟiyappaṭutalāl, itaṉaik kāṭciyaṟi veṉṟum kūṟuvar. paṇṭai
   nikaḻccikaḷai niralē eṭuttukkēṭkum vēntaṉ maṉam teruṇṭu aruḷaṟam
   mēṟkoḷḷattakka neṟiyiṉkaṇ vaittu ōtukiṉṟā ṉeṉpār "poruḷataṉai vāṅki
   avaṉuṇarum vakai vaittaruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉ" eṉṟār. aṟinta,
   aṟiyappaṭuvatāyirunta veṉpatu. amarulakattai intirapaṭalameṉṟum, aḵtu
   aṟupattu mūṉṟu vakaippaṭumeṉṟum kūṟupavātaliṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi leytiya
   acōkaṉai "ōṅkupuka ḻamarulaka moṉṟiṉu ḷuvantāṉ" eṉṟār,. "antarat
   taṟupattu mūṉṟatākiya intirap paṭalamum"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
   nalviṉai yāṟṟip pukaḻeytiṉō rulaka meṉṟaṟku "ōṅku pukaḻamarulakam"
   eṉṟāreṉa vuṇarka. uvantāṉ eṉa iṟanta kālattāṟ kūṟiyataṉāl mutaṟkaṇ
   eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu.
   ---------------
       1. mēru 91.

   acōkaṉ piṉpu piramakaṟpattil iṉitiruttal
   arumaṇiyi ṉoḷitikaḻu mamaraṉava ṉākip
       piramaṉula kataṉuṇmikai peṟukaṭalkaḷ pattum
       tirumaṇiya tuṇaimulaiya teyvamaṭa vārō
       ṭarumaiyila ṉakamakiḻvi ṉamarumavaṉ* mātō.         285
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *makiḻviṉ maruvumaṉ

   urai:- avaṉ-acōkaṉ, arumaṇiyiṉ oḷi tikaḻum amaraṉavaṉ
   āki-kiṭaittaṟkariya māṇikka maṇiyiṉatu oḷi vīcum tēvaṉāki, piramaṉ
   ulakataṉuḷ-piṉpu piramakaṟpattil, mikaipeṟu kaṭalkaḷ
   pattum-mikutiyākappeṟṟa vāḻ nāṭkaḷ pattukkaṭaṟkālam, tirumaṇiya tuṇai
   mulaiya teyva maṭavārōṭu-aḻakiya maṇimālai yaṇinta iraṇṭākiya
   mulaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya teyvamakaḷiruṭaṉ, arumaiyilaṉ-pōka nukarccikkaṇ
   arumai ciṟitu miṉṟi mikka, akamakiḻviṉ amarum-maṉamakiḻcciyōṭu
   virumpiyuṟaivāṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   tēvarkaḷ cemmaṇipōlum niṟamuṭaiya mēṉiyarākaliṉ, "arumaṇiyiṉ oḷi
   tikaḻum amaraṉavaṉāki" eṉṟār. "oḷiyumiḻn tilaṅku mēṉi paritiyi ṉiyaṉṟa
   tokkum"2 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟuvar. tēvarulakattuc cautarma kaṟpamutalākak
   kūṟappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, nāṉku laṭcam vimāṉaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya piramakaṟpattil
   acōkaṉ iruntāṉ eṉka.
   ----------
       2 cīvaka 2800

   mūvakai yōkaṅkaḷum muṟṟi nālvakai ārātaṉai tiyāṉattāl uṭampu nīṅki
   amararulaku pukuvōr piramakaṟpam pukuvareṉpatu [1] caiṉa nūṉ muṭipu.
   pirama piramōttara kaṟpa tēvarkaḷukku āyuḷ pattukkaṭalākum. kaṭaleṉpatu
   caiṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇum kāla aḷavu. "ayaṉam āṇṭu, paṇaiyukam pūvam palla
   pavvamē yaṉanta mīṟāk, kaṇmutaṟ kālapētam" [2] eṉa varuvatu kāṇka.
   makaḷi riṉpattil tiḷaittukkoṇṭiruttalai, "muṉceynal viṉaiyiṉāl mukiliṉ
   miṉṉaṉār, iṉceyvāyavarēntu koṅkaiyar, vantiṭaic cūḻntiṭa vaṇaṅka
   vāṉavar, antami liṉpattu ḷamaraṉ mēviṉāṉ" [3] eṉṟu mērumantara purāṇam
   kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. tirumaṇiya, tuṇaimulaiya eṉpaṉa kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷ.
   makaḷiriṉpam eḷitiṟ peṟuvataṉṟākaliṉ, ataṉai iṉitu eḷitiṟ peṟum
   acōkaṉai "arumaiyilaṉ" eṉṟār; "añcol maṭavārtam mārvak kaḷipoṅka,
   neñcat tayilēṟṟum nīḷveṅ kaḻuvūrntum, kuñcik kaḷiyāṉaik kōṭṭaḻa
   luṭpaṭṭum, tuñciṟ ṟulakantō tuṉpak kaṭaluḷḷē" [4] eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu
   kāṇka. mātum ovum acai.
   ------------
       1. mēruman. 375. 2. mēru. 94.
       3. mēru. 511. 4. cīvaka. 2792.

   amirtamatiyiṉ piṟappuneṟi kūṟutal
   vañcaṉaiyi laṉṉaiyuṭaṉ maṉṉavaṉai nañciṉ*
       tuñcumvakai cūḻntutoḻu nōymuḻutu māki
       añciṉmoḻi yamirtamati yarunarakiṉ vīḻntāḷ
       nañcaṉaiya viṉainaliya nāmanakai vēlōy.         286
       ----------
       (pāṭam.)* yeñca

   urai:- nāma nakai vēlōy - accam poruntiya oḷi vīcum vēlaiyuṭaiya vēntē,
   vañcaṉaiyil - vañcaṉaiyāka, maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉai,
   aṉṉaiyuṭaṉ - avaṉ tāyākiya cantiramatiyuṭaṉē, nañcil tuñcum vakai
   cūḻntu - viṭattāl iṟakkumāṟu uṇpittu, toḻu nōy muḻutumāki - uṭal
   muḻutum kuṭṭanō yuṟṟu, arunarakil - nīkkutaṟkariya narakattilē,
   nañcaṉaiya viṉai naliya - tāṉ uṇpitta viṭattaippōlum tīviṉaiyāṉatu
   varutta, am cil moḻi yamirtamati - aḻakiya cilavāya coṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyaḷāṉa
   amirtamatiyāṉavāḷ, vīḻntāḷ - vīḻntu varuntavāḷāyiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   vañcaṉaiyāka nañcūṭṭit taṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉaiyum avaṉtāy
   cantiramatiyaiyum koṉṟataik kuṟittu, "nañcil tuñcum vakai cūḻntu"
   eṉṟār. kāraṇam kāriyamāka upacarikkap paṭṭatu. acceyaliṉ viḷaivu toḻu
   nōyāy immaiyil varuttiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟku, "toḻunōy muḻutumāki" eṉṟum,
   maṟumaiyil narakaṭaintā ḷeṉṟaṟku, "nañcaṉaiya viṉainaliya" "arunarakiṉ
   viḻntāḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār."añciṉmoḻi yamirtamati" yeṉṟatu kuṟippāl colliṟ
   ciṉṉamaiyuṟiṉum tīya niṉaivu ceyalkaḷiṟ paṉmaiyuṇmai tōṟṟuvittu ikaḻvu
   pulappaṭuttiṟṟu.
   narakavakaikaḷai muṉivaṉ vēntaṟkuk kūṟutal
   iruḷiṉiru ḷiruḷpukaiyo ṭaḷaṟumaṇal paraliṉ
       maruḷceyuru viṉaporuḷiṉ varupeyaru mavaiyē
       veruḷceyviṉai tarutuyaram viḷaiyunila micaippat*
       teruḷiyeḻu+ vakainaraka kuḻikaḷivai tārōy.         287
       ------------------
       (pāṭam) *micaiyat. + teruḷiṉeḻu.

   urai:-tārōy - mālaiyai yaṇinta vēntē, veruḷ ceyviṉai tarutuyaram -
   mayakkattaicceyyum tiviṉaikaḷ payakkum tuṉpam, viḷaiyum nilam icaippat
   teruḷi - vantu varuttum iṭaṅkaḷai yām collakkēṭṭut teḷintu koḷvāyāka,
   naraka kuḻikaḷ ivai - narakakkuḻikaḷākiya ivai, eḻuvakai -
   ēḻuvakaippaṭum, iruḷiṉ iruḷ - tamat tamappirapai, iruḷ -
   tamappirapai,pukai- tūmappirapai, aḷaṟu -paṅkap pirapai maṇal - vālulap
   pirapai, paraliṉ - paraṟkaṟkaḷākiya carkkarāp pirapai maruḷcey uruviṉ
   poruḷiṉ-kaṇṭārkku mayakkattaic ceyyam irattiṉaṅkaḷ niṟainta
   arataṉappirapaiyeṉa varupeyarum avai - varum peyarum avaṟṟiṟku avaiyē
   yām e-ṟu.
   oḷiyālum vaṉmaiyālum paṉmaiyālum taṉpālaṭaintārkku arataṉam pōlap
   perumayakkattaic ceytaliṉ, arataṉap pirapaiyai, "maruḷce yuruviṉa
   poruḷiṉ" eṉṟār. uruviṉa eṉpataiyē arataṉappirapaikku ēṟṟi, poruḷiṉ
   eṉpataṟku iruḷiṉ iruḷ mutaliyaṉa tamattamappirapai mutaliyavaṟṟil
   uḷḷaporuḷ ipporuḷkaḷē eṉṟu uraittalumām. pirapai, vaṭṭam. ōṭu, eṇṇōṭu.
   iṉ, cāriyai. veruḷ, mayakkam; "veruḷi māntar" 1eṉṟāṟpōla. teruḷi,
   muṉṉilaikkaṇ vantatu. ikaram, vikuti.piṟāṇṭum "tiṉṉi" (293) eṉṟataṟkum
   ituvē kūṟikkoḷka; ivai teruṭi, tiṉṟiyeṉa niṟṟaṟpālaṉa. iṅkē kūṟiya
   narakavakaikaḷai, "ēḻuḷa narakam nāma mirataṉañ carkkavālu, vāḻiya
   paṅkan tūman tamantamat tamattamākum" 2 eṉpataṉālumuṇarka.ivaṟṟiṉ
   iyalpai yaṟintāl yacōmati tīviṉai ceyya niṉaiyāṉ eṉpatukoṇṭu,
   "icaippatteruḷi" yeṉṟār; piṟacāṉṟōrum, "māṟṟutaṟkariya tuṉpam peri
   teṉṟu mayaṅkavēṇṭā, māṟṟutaṟ keḷitu kīḻkīḻ narakattavviyal paṟintāl" 3
   eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
   ------------
       1. cīvaka. 73. 2. mēru. 936. 3.mēru.935.

   amirtamati tūmappirapaiyil vīḻntu varuntuntiṟattai muṉivaṉ kūṟal.
   mērukiri yuyttiṭiṉum veppamoṭu taṭpa
       nīreṉavu rukkiṭuni lappuraiya* vaintām
       oriṉuṟu pukainaraki ṉurukiyuṭaṉ vīḻntāḷ
       ārumila ḷaṟaṉumila ḷamirtamati yavaḷē. ++         288
       --------------
       (pāṭam.) * nivappuṭaiya ++ yaṉṟē.

   urai:- amirtamatiyavaḷ - amirtamati yākiya attīviṉaiyāṭṭi, oriṉ -
   ārāyumiṭattu, ārum ilaḷ - annarakil taṉakku oppāvār oruttarumiṉṟi,
   aṟaṉum ilaḷ - aṟattiṉ tuṇaitāṉum iṉṟi, mērukiri yuyttiṭiṉum -
   mērumalaiyaik koṇarntu taṉkaṇ iṭṭālum, veppamoṭu - veppattāl, taṭpa
   nīreṉa urukkiṭum - kuḷircciyai yuṭaiya nīrpōla urukkiviṭattakka,
   nilappuraiya - nilaṅkaḷākiya puraikaḷaiyuṭaiya, aintām pukaiyuṟu
   narakiṉ - aintāvatākiya pukaimikka tūmappirapai yeṉṉum narakil,
   urukiyuṭaṉ vīḻntāḷ - vīḻntu vēkalāṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   paṉimūṭik kuḷircci mikuntu uṟaippuṇṭu calippiṉṟit tiṇṇitāyirukkum
   malaikaṭkut talaiyāya malaiyāyiṉum, mērumalai nilaipeyarntu taṉkaṇ
   vīḻntāl, taṉ veppattāl ataṉai nīrāy urukkiviṭum eṉṟatu, tūmappirapaiyiṉ
   veppamikuti yuṇarttiyavāṟu, narakamovvoṉṟiṟkum palapuraika ḷuṇmaiyiṉ,
   "puraiya" eṉṟār. itaṉiyalpai, "mērunē riruppu vaṭṭai yiṭṭa vakkaṇatti
   ṉuḷḷē, nīreṉa vurukkuñ cītaveppaṅ kaṇiṉṟakīḻmēl, ārvami laṟivaṉ tanta
   nūliṉain tāvataṉṉiṟ, kārmukil vaṇṇa cīta veppaṅka ṇiṉṟa kaṇṭāy"1
   eṉpataṉāl uṇarntu koḷka. amirtamatikku nikarāka mikka tīviṉai ceytār
   piṟar ilar eṉpataṟku "ārumilaḷ" eṉṟum, aṟam ciṟitēṉum iruntiruppiṉum
   akkoṭiya naraku pukutaṟku ētu irātu eṉpār, "aṟaṉumilaḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   amirtamatiyākiya avaḷ eṉpuḻic cuṭṭu,avaḷ ceyta tīviṉaiyaic cuṭṭi
   niṉṟatu.
   -------------
       1. mēru. 945.

   avaḷeytiya narakavētaṉaiyaik kūṟutal
   āḻntakuḻi vīḻntapoḻu tarunaraka rōṭic
       cūḻntutukai yāveriyu ḷiṭṭaṉarkaḷ cuṭṭār
       pōḻntaṉarkaḷ puṇperuka vaṉṟaṟipu ṭaittār
       mūḻntaviṉai muṉiyumeṉiṉ muṉiyalaru muḷarō.         289

   urai:-- āḻnta kuḻi vīḻntapoḻutu - āḻamāṉa annarakakkuḻiyil avaḷ
   vīḻntapōtu, arunarakar - vilakkaṟkariya narakavīrar, ōṭi - ōṭippōy
   avaḷaip piṭittu, cūḻntu - palarāykkūṭi, tukaiyā - utaittumitittu,
   eriyuḷ iṭṭaṉarkaḷ cuṭṭār - neruppiṉiṭṭut cuṭṭārkaḷ, puṇperuka
   pōḻntaṉarkaḷ - avaḷuṭaliṟ puṇṇuṇṭāmāṟu vāḷāṟ piḷantārkaḷ, vaṉtaṟi
   puṭaittār - valiya kampattāl avaḷai yaṭittārkaḷ, mūḻnta viṉai - tām
   ceytumuṟṟiya viṉai, muṉiyumeṉiṉ - tammai veṟuttup pakaikkumāyiṉ,
   muṉiyalarum uḷarō - veṟātavarum uṇṭō, illai, e - ṟu.
   aintām narakamātaliṉ, āḻntakuḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu. narakat tuṉpam
   kaḻiyuṅkālat taṉṟip piṟāṇṭu evvaḻiyum vilakkuṟātu oṟukkum iyalpiṉarātal
   tōṉṟa "arunarakar" eṉṟār. tīyiṭṭuk koḷuttalum, vāḷāṟ pōḻtalum,
   taṟiyāṟpuṭaittalum niralē kūṟiyatu, avaḷuṟṟa tuṉpam kūṟumāṟṟāl
   yacōmatiyai naṉ ṉeṟippaṭuttaṟku; avaṉ maṉattai varuttutaṟ kaṉṟu; "aruḷ
   vaḷarkkum coṉṉavil cutattamuṉi" atu ceyyāreṉavuṇarka. tammuyirkku iṉpan
   tarumeṉac ceyta tīviṉai atu tārātu tuṉpantarutaliṉ, "mūḻnta viṉai
   muṉiyiṉ" eṉṟum, oruvaṟku avaṉ ceyviṉaipōlap payaṉ viḷaippatil oruvarum
   nikarātal muṭiyāmaiyiṉ ataṉiṟ cuṟṟam cīriyatu piṟitillai yeṉṟum,
   accuṟṟamillārkkuc cuṟṟameṉpatē illaiyeṉṟum kūṟuvār. "muṉiyalarum uḷarō"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. muḻuteṉṉum col mūḻtteṉa viṉaiyāki, etukainōkki melintu
   niṉṟatu. ummai, ciṟappu. ōkāram, etirmaṟai.

   centacaikaḷ ventaṉakaṉ tiṉṟaṉaimu ṉeṉṟē
       kontaḻalil ventukotu kotuveṉako tikkum
       centaḻali ṉintirarkaḷ cempukaḷ tiṇippa
       ventaḻali ṉainturuki* viṇṭoḻuku mukaṉē.         290
       -------------
       (pāṭam)*(turukum)

   urai:-muṉ - iṟappataṟku muṉṉē, centacaikaḷ ventaṉakaḷ tiṉṟaṉaiyē eṉṟu -
   cevviya vūṉkaḷai vēkavaittut tiṉṟā yaṉṟē eṉṟu colli yikaḻntu,
   centaḻaliṉ intirarkaḷ - civanta neruppaippōlum talaivarkaḷ,
   korantaḻalil ventu - kottāka mikkeḻum neruppil ventu, kotu kotu veṉak
   kotikkum - kotu kotu veṉṟu kotikkiṉṟa, cempukaḷ tiṇippa - cepput
   tuṇṭaṅḷaiyeṭuttu avaḷ vāyiliṭṭut tiṇittārkaḷāka, ventaḻaliṉ - avaṟṟiṉ
   mikkuṟṟa veppattāl, naintu uruki viṇṭu mukaṉ oḻukum - avaḷuṭaiya mukam
   ventu nīrāyurukit talaiyiṉiṉṟum oḻukuvatāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.
   narakavīrarkaṭkut talaivar neruppuppōlum niṟamum vemmaippaṇpum
   uṭaiyārātal tōṉṟa, "centaḻaliṉ intirarkaḷ" eṉṟār. intirar, talaivar;
   ivarkaḷ narakalōkattē vāḻum narakavīrarkaṭkut talaivarpōlum.
   cepputtaṭikaḷ ūṉtaṭipōla iruttaliṉ avaṭku ūṉuṇavāka ivarkaḷ avaṟṟaik
   kotikkak kotikka vēkavaittu vāyil tiṇittārkaḷ eṉka. avveppa mikutiyai
   yāṟṟāmaiyāl mukam uruki naintāḷ eṉpār, "ven taḻaliṉ nainturuki
   viṇṭoḻukum mukaṉē" eṉṟār. eṉṟē yeṉpuḻi ēkāram pirittu kūṭṭappaṭṭatu.
   mukaṉē eṉpuḻiyaḵtu īṟṟacai.

   karukakaruka rintaṉaḷ kaviṉkoḷoru* pāvai
       perukeriyi ṉiṭṭuruka vituperitu miṉiteṉ+
       ṟarukaṇaiya nutantutalu malaṟiyatu taḻuvip
       poruporupo rintupoṭi yāmuṭala mellām.         291
       ------------
       (pāṭam)*ḷuruviṉeru; kaviṉuru; karuviṉeru.
       +peritumiṉitē yeṉṟu, mituvumiṉitē yeṉṟu

   urai:-karukaru karintaṉaḷ - narakattīyāl mikak karu karuttut
   tīyntavaḷāṉa amirtamatiyai, kaviṉkoḷ oru pāvai-aḻukukoḷḷacceyta oru āṇ
   pāvaiyaikkāṭṭi, peruku eriyiṉ iṭṭu - ataṉai mikkeḻunterinta tīyiliṭṭuk
   kāyavaittu, uruka - uruki nekiḻum patattil, itu peritum iṉitu eṉṟu -
   itu taḻuvutaṟku uṉakku iṉitāyirukkumākaliṉ taḻuvuka eṉṟu colli, aruku
   aṇaiya nuntutalum - ataṉarukēceṉṟu taḻuvumāṟu narakavīrar
   avaḷaittaḷḷavum, alaṟi - ōlamiṭṭaḻutu, atu taḻuvi- ataṉai yavar
   colliyapaṭi taḻuvi, uṭalam ellām - avaḷ uṭampellām, poru poru porintu -
   poruporu veṉap porintu, poṭiyām - poṭiyāy viḻukiṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
   muṉpē, "eriyuḷ iṭṭaṉar cuṭṭār"(289) kaḷātaliṉ ventu karinatukiṭanta
   avaḷ nilaiyiṉai, "karukaru karintaṉaḷ" eṉṟār. piṟaṉai viḻaintu aṟam
   piḻaittāḷātaliṉ, appāvaṅ kuṟittu oṟuppār, aḻakiya āṭavaṉoruvaṉaippōla
   uruvamaittu neruppiliṭṭuc civakkak kāycci ataṉait taḻuvuka eṉṟu naraka
   vīrar uraittapotu, avaḷ añciniṉṟāḷāka atukaṇṭu vekuṇṭu ataṉai avaḷait
   taḻuvumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyatu tōṉṟa, "itu peritum iṉiteṉṟu arukaṇaiya
   nuntutalum" eṉṟār. aṭṭapaṅkaṉatu kūṭṭattai virumpi yoḻukiya niṉakku itu
   mika iṉitāmeṉpār, "itu peritum iṉitu" eṉṟu narakavīrar collip paḻittār
   eṉṟār. ataṉait taḻuviyataṉ payaṉāka, avaḷuṭalam muḻuvatum ventu
   poṭiyāyiṟ ṟeṉpār, ittuṇai yoṟuttalukkum avaḷuṭal uyiriṉ nīṅkātu
   oṉṟiyiruttaliṉ, avvoṟṟumai kuṟittu "poṭiyām" eṉṟār. karukaru, poru poru
   eṉpaṉa iraṭṭaikkiḷavikaḷ; muṉṉē kotu kotu veṉṟatu matu.
   avaḷai nañcuṇpittamai kūṟal
   nāvaḻuki vīḻamuḻu* nañcuṇa maṭuttār
       āvalaṟi yatuvuruki yalamariṉu maṉṉō
       cāvavari tivaṇaraci takavilviṉai tarunōy
       yāvumviḷai nilamataṉi liṉiyavuḷa vāmō.         292
       -----------
       (pāṭam.)*vīḻmuḻutu

   urai:- nā aḻuki vīḻa-nā mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷ aḻuki vīḻa, muḻunañcu
   uṇa-avaṟṟai kalappillāta koṭiya nañcu kalantu uṇpippārāy, atu uruki
   alaṟi alamariṉum-ataṉaikkaṇṭu maṉaṅkalaṅkik kataṟi yaḻutu nāṟ puṟamum
   cuḻaṉṟōṭiṉum, maṭuttār-avaḷaiviṭātu paṟṟi uṇpittārkaḷ, aṉṉō-aiyō, ivaṇ
   araci-in narakattē paṇṭu innilattē araciyāyirunta amirtamati, cāva
   aritu-cātal iṉṟi nañciṉāl viḷaiyum tuṉpattai nukarntuṟaiyalāṉāḷ,
   takavil viṉai tarum nōy yāvum viḷainilam-takutiyillāta tīviṉaikaḷ yāvum
   payakkum tuṉpamellām vantu varuttum iṭamākiya itaṉkaṇ, iṉiya
   uḷavāmō-iṉiya iṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭō, illai yaṉṟō e-ṟu.
   eṉavē avaḷ nāṟpuṟamum oṭi alamaruvatu eṟṟukku eṉa iraṅkiyavāṟu. ā,
   irakkak kuṟippuṇarttum iṭaiccol. avaḷuṭaliṉiṉṟu aḻuki vīḻntavaṟṟai
   avaḷaiyē uṇṇacceyyum koṭumaiyuṭaṉ, avaṟṟait tūya nañciṟ kalan tuṇpikkum
   koṭumaikaṇṭu, avaḷ varuntum tiṟattai, " ā alaṟiyatu vuruki yalamariṉum"
   eṉṟār. tīyiliṭṭum, vāḷāṟ pōḻntum, nañcuṇ pittum varuttiya kālattum avaḷ
   uyir tuṟavāmaikku ētu kūṟuvār, "cāvavaritu" eṉṟum, ivvulakil
   araciyiyiruntāḷāyiṉum, narakil peruntuṉpam uṟuvatu orutalai yeṉṟaṟku
   "ivaṇaraci" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "eṟivem paṭaiyā livarvīḻn teḻalāl, uṟuven
   tuyaralla tuṭampu viṭār"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutalāl cātal aritātal kāṇka.
   tāṉ ceyta viṉaippayaṉai nukarumiṭamātaliṉ, "iṉiya vuḷavāmō" eṉṟār;
   "viṉaiyē tuyarattai viḷaippa talāl, niṉaivār ceyal maṟṟilai"2 eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭuvatum īṇṭu aṟiyattakkatu.
   avaḷuṟṟa tuṉpaṅkaḷai mēlum kūṟal.
   muṉṉainumar tantacai muṉintilai nukarttāyk
       kiṉṟumiṉi tuṉṉavaya vaṅkaḷtiṉa leṉṟē
       taṉṉavaya vampalata ṭintuḻala vaittu
       tiṉṉiyeṉa nontavaikaḷ tiṉṉumikai tiṟalōy.         293

   urai :- mikai tiṟalōy - mikattiṟaluṭaiya aracē, muṉṉai numartam tacai
   muṉintilai nukarntāykku -muṉṉē niṉ kaṇavaṉum māmiyumākiyōruṭaiya ūṉai
   veṟātuṇṭa uṉakku, uṉ avayaṅkaḷ tiṉal - uṉṉuṭaiya uṟup pukkaḷaiyē nī
   tiṉpatu, iṉṉum iṉitu - ippoḻutum iṉitākavē yirukkum, eṉṟu - eṉṟu colli,
   taṉ avayavam pala uḻalat taṭintu vaittu - avaḷuṭaiya uṟuppukkaḷai avaḷ
   tuṭituṭikka aṟuttu etirē vaittu, tiṉṉi eṉa - tiṉpāyāka eṉṟu oṟukkavē,
   nontu - maṉam varunti, avaikaḷ tiṉṉum - avaṟṟait tiṉpāḷāyiṉāḷ. e-ṟu.
   yacōtaraṉum cantiramatiyum mīṉākavum makarākavum piṟavākavum,
   piṟantuḻaṉṟapōtu avaṟṟaikkoṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉait tiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, ataṉaicu
   cuṭṭi, "muṉṉai numartam tacai muṉintilai tiṉṟāykku" eṉṟum, avarūṉum niṉ
   uṭalūṉum oṉṟēyātaliṉ, appōtu ataṉaiyuṇṭa niṉakku ippōtum ivai
   iṉiyavākavē yirukkumātaliṉ uṇka eṉpār, "iṉṉum iṉitu uṉṉavayavaṅkaḷ
   tiṉal" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. avayavaṅkaḷait tuṇṭu tuṇṭāka aṟutteṭutta kālattu
   āṟṟāmaiyāl avaḷ varuntiṉamai tōṉṟa, "uḻalattaṭintu" eṉṟum, avaṟṟaik
   kaṇṭu aruvaruppu mikakkoḷḷumāṟu, "vaittu" eṉṟum, avar kūṟumāṟu uṇṭaṟku
   avaṭku uḷḷam cellāmaitōṉṟa, "tiṉṉiyeṉa" eṉṟum, "nontavaikaḷ tiṉṉum"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

       1. mēru. 942. 2. mēru. 943.

   tilappoṟiyi liṭṭaṉar tirippuvu neruppiṉ
       ulaipperu kaḻaṟṟalai yurukkavu muruttuk
       kolaikkaḻuvi liṭṭaṉar kulaippavu muḻakkum
       ulaipparuva ruttama turaipparitu kaṇṭāy.         294

   urai:-- tilappoṟiyil iṭṭaṉar tirippavum - eḷḷaiyāṭṭum entiramākiya
   cekkiliṭṭu av vamirtamatiyait tuṉpuṟuttiyum, neruppiṉ ulai - neruppil
   vaikkum ulaippāṉaiyil ēṟṟi, peruku aḻaltalai urukkavum - mikkeḻum
   neruppiṉiṭattē vaittu urukkiyum, uruttu - vekuṇṭu, kolaikkaḻuvi
   liṭṭaṉar kulaippavum - kolai puriyum kaḻumarattilēṟṟi avaḷuruvai
   yaḻittum varutta, uḻakkum - varuntum, ulaipparu varuttamatu -
   pōkkutaṟkariya avaḷuṭaiya tuṉpamāṉatu, uraipparitu - collavoṇṇātatām
   e-ṟu.
   eḷḷaippeytu āṭṭi eṇṇey eṭukkum poṟi, cekku. neruppil vaittu
   urukkutaṟkeṉṟē camaintuḷḷa urukkup pāṉaikaḷai, "neruppiṉ ulai" eṉṟār.
   kolaittoḻiṟ keṉṟamaintamaiyiṉ kaḻuvaik "kolaikkaḻu" eṉpārāyiṟṟu; ataṉāl
   uruccitaintu pōtaliṉ, "kulaippavum" eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ulaippu - keṭuttal,
   nīkkutal, kaṇṭāy, acai.

   orupatiṉo ṭorupatiṉai yuntiyata ṉumpar
       irupatiṉo ṭaintiṉi luyarntapukai yeṉṉum
       poruvariya tuyariṉavai poṅkiyuṭaṉ vīḻum
       orupatiṉo ṭeḻukaṭalka ḷaḷavumoḷi tārōy.         295

   urai:-oḷi tārōy.- oḷi poruntiya mālaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vēntē, orupatiṉōṭu
   orupatiṉai yunti- orupattōṭu oru pattiṉaip peytu perukki, ataṉ umpar
   iru patiṉoṭu aintiṉil- varum nūṟuṭaṉ ataṉmēl irupattaintukūṭa uṇṭākiya
   nūṟṟirupattaintāka, uyarnta pukaiyeṉṉum aḷavu - uyariya pukaiyeṉṟu
   collap paṭukiṉṟa aḷavil, poruvariya tuyariṉavai- oppillāta
   tuṉpavuṭampukaḷaip peṟṟu, orupatiṉoṭu eḻukaṭalkaḷ aḷavum-
   patiṉēḻukaṭalkaḷ kālam, poṅki uṭaṉvīḻum - uyarntu vaḷarntu vīḻvāṉāyiṉāḷ
   e-ṟu.
   eṉṟatu, ivvaintām narakattil puraitōṟum tāṉpeṟṟa narakavuṭampu uyarntu
   muṭivil nūṟṟirupattaintu pukai yeṉṉum aḷavukoṇṭa uyaram uyarntu maṭivāḷ
   eṉṟum, ataṉkāla ellai patiṉēḻu kaṭalkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyavāṟām. pukai
   yeṉpatu nīṭṭalaḷavaiyil oṉṟu; pukai yōcaṉaiyumām. kaṭal, kāla
   aḷavaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. mutalām narakattil narakaratu uṭampu ēḻē mukkālē
   vīcam pukai yeṉṟum, aḵtu narakantōṟum iraṭṭit tuyarntu aintām
   narakattiṟutiyil nūṟṟirupattaintu pukaiyumām uyarameṉṟum kūṟupa. "oṉṟu
   mūṉṟaintu mēḻu moṉpatum pattō ṭoṉṟum, niṉṟamūṉ ṟōṭu pattu nirayattup
   puraikaḷ mēṉmēl. oṉṟumūṉ ṟēḻu pattum orupattēḻ irupattīriṉ,
   niṉṟamūṉṟōṭu muppāṉāḻikīḻ puraitōṟāyu"1 eṉpataṉāl, aintā narakil vāḻnā
   ḷaḷavu patiṉēḻu kaṭalkaḷāta luṇarka.

       1.mēru. 937.

   tollaiviṉai niṉṟucuṭu kiṉṟanara kattuḷ
       allalivai yallaṉavu mamiḻtamati yuṟuvaḷ
       ellaiyila yituviteṉa veṇṇiyoru nāviṟ
       collavula vāvoḻika cuṭaruneṭu muṭiyōy.         296

   urai:- cuṭarum neṭu muṭiyōy- oḷirkiṉṟa neṭiya muṭiyaiyuṭaiya aracaṉē,
   tollaiviṉainiṉṟu cuṭukiṉṟa narakattuḷ allal ivai- paṇṭaiviṉaiyāṉatu
   viṭātu paṟṟi varuttum narakattil tuṉpaṅkaḷ ivaikaḷākum, allaṉavum
   amiḻtamatiyuṟuvaḷ - amiḻtamati yivaiyēyaṉṟi ivaiyallāta vēṟupiṟa
   tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum aṭaivāḷ, ellaiyila -avai ellaiyillātaṉavākiya avaṟṟai,
   itu itu eṉa eṇṇi- ovvoṉṟāka iḵtu ittaṉmaittu eṉṟu eṇṇi, orunāvil colla
   ulavā - oru nāvāṟ colvataṟku illai, oḻika - iṉi avaṟṟaikkēṭṭalai yoḻika
   e-ṟu.
   muṉṉaip piṟappiṟ ceyta tīviṉaip payaṉai narakattiṭattē yiruntu
   nukarvatu kuṟittu, "tollaiviṉai niṉṟu cuṭukiṉṟa narakattuḷ " eṉṟār.
   cuṭukiṉṟa teṉṟatu, tuṉpam ceyvatupaṟṟi; "tīyavai tīyiṉu mañcappaṭum?
   eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvap peruntakai kūṟiyatu kāṇka. makkaṭ piṟappil
   viṉaippayaṉ kaṇantōṟum māṟi varutal pōlātu narakakatiyil māṟutaliṉṟi
   orupaṭittāy niṉṟu varuttuvatu paṟṟi, "niṉṟu cuṭukiṉṟa narakam" eṉṟāreṉa
   vuṇarka. itukāṟum kūṟiya tuṉpaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟāp pala tuṉ paṅka
   ḷuṇṭeṟku "allaṉavum amiḻtamati yuṟuvaḷ" eṉṟār. avai ōrallaik
   kuṭpaṭuvaṉa vallavātaliṉ, "ellaiyila" eṉappaṭṭaṉa' ataṉal avaṟṟai
   vakuttum virittum ōtuvatu eḷitaṉṟeṉṟum, payaṉil ceyaleṉṟum kūṟuvār,
   "ituvitu veṉṟu orunāviṟ collavulavā" eṉṟum, "oḻika" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   yacōtaraṉum cantiramatiyum piṟantu varuntiya tiṟattaik kūṟutal
   eṇṇami licōtaraṉo ṭaṉṉaiyivar muṉṉāḷ
       kaṇṇiya vuyirkkolai viṉaikkoṭumai yālē
       naṇṇiya vilaṅkiṭai naṭuṅkañar toṭarnta
       vaṇṇamitu vaṭivamivai vaḷaroḷiya pūṇōy.         297

   urai:- vaḷar oḷiya pūṇōy - perukukiṉṟa oḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya aṇikaḷaiyaṇinta
   vēntē, eṇṇam il icōtaraṉōṭu aṉṉaiyivar - piṉ viḷaivu niṉaiyāta
   yacōtaraṉum tāyākiya cantiramatiyumākiya ivviruvarum, muṉṉāḷ kaṇṇiya
   uyirkkolai viṉaikkoṭumaiyāl - muṉṉai nāḷil uyirppali yīṭākak karutic
   ceyta uyiraik kolvatākiya tīviṉaiyiṉ tīmaiyāl, naṇṇiya vilaṅkiṭai-
   uṇṭākiya vilaṅkukatikkaṇṇē, naṭuṅku añar toṭarnta vaṇṇam itu-
   naṭuṅkattakka tuṉpam vantu paṟṟi varuttiya vakai yituvākum, vaṭivam
   ivai - eṭutta uṭampukaḷum ivaiyākum e-ṟu.
   "ayirppateṉ ṉaṟattiṉ tiṇmai yaṟivataṟ kamaivi lātāṉ" (144) eṉṟu muṉpu
   kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, īṇṭu, "eṇṇa mil icōtaraṉ" eṉṟār. cantiramatiyaiyum
   ivvāṟē "tiruvili" (142) eṉṟum, "avvaiyāya pāvi" (145) eṉṟum
   ikaḻntamaiyiṉ, īṅku vāḷā "aṉṉai" yeṉṟoḻintār. uyirkkolaiyākiya
   viṉaiyaic ceytaṟkaṇ, yacōtaraṉ iyaṟṟum viṉai mutalāyiṉamaiyiṉ, ammikuti
   kuṟittu, oṭuviṉai yavaṉmēl vaitturaittār. oṭu, eṇṇupporuṭṭu. "muṉṉē
   tuñcā vilaṅkiṭait tuṉṉiṉār" (153) eṉṟamaiyiṉ, iṅkē, "viṉaikkoṭumaiyālē
   naṇṇiya vilaṅkiṭai" yeṉṟum, akkati, "tuyaram tuñcā vilaṅku" (153)
   eṉṟataṟkēṟpa, "naṭuṅku añar toṭarnta vaṇṇam" eṉṟum, ataṉ vakaiyum
   piṟavum iṉik kūṟaluṟutaliṉ, "vaṇṇamitu vaṭivamivai" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itu
   tokuttuc cuṭṭal.
   yacōtaraṉuṟṟa piṟappu vakaiyaik kūṟutal
   maṉṉaṉ mayilāymayiri muḷḷeyiṉa mīṉāy
       piṉṉirumu ṟaittakaru mākiyava ṉēki
       maṉṉuciṟai vāraṇama tākivata maruvi
       maṉṉavaniṉ makaṉapaya ṉākivaḷar kiṉṟāṉ.         298

   urai:-maṉṉava-vēntē, maṉṉaṉ - aracaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉ, mayil - mayil
   paṟavaiyākavum, mayiri muḷ eyiṉam- mayir mikka muḷḷam paṉṟiyākavum,
   mīṉāy - mīṉākavum, piṉ irumuṟait takarumākiyavaṉ- piṉṉar iraṇṭumuṟai
   āṭākavum piṟantu varuntiṉavaṉāy, ēki - puḷvakaiyuṭceṉṟu, maṉṉuciṟai
   vāraṇamatāki - periya ciṟakukaḷaiyuṭaiya kōḻiyāki, vatam maruvi -
   paṉṉiruvakai virataṅkaḷaiyum kēṭṭu, niṉ makaṉ apayaṉāki vaḷarkiṉṟāṉ.-
   niṉmakaṉākiya apayaruciyākap piṟantu vaḷarntu varukiṉṟāṉ e-ṟu.
   mayiṟpiṟappukkup piṉ "cūḻmayir muḷḷuṭai yiṉṉal ceyyumōr ēṉama tāki "
   (176)p piṟantamaiyiṉ, "mayiri muḷḷeyiṉam" eṉṟār. mayiruṭaiyatu, mayiri.
   ēṉameṉpatu eyiṉameṉa niṉṟatu. mīṉ piṟappukkup piṉ ṉoru muṟaiyum,ap
   piṟappiṟ kūṭiya maṟiyāṭṭiṉ vayiṟṟilē karuvāy maṟumuṟaiyum āṭāyp

       (pāṭam) *viṉaikaḷ viḷaiyumiyalpu †mikai

   urai:- numarkaḷ pavam itu-uṉṉuṭaiya muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ piṟappuvakai ituvākum,
   viṉaitaṉ viḷaivum itu- appiṟappukku ētuvākiya viṉaiyiṉ payaṉum
   ituvākum, iyalpum itu-avviṉaippayaṉākavarum katiyiṉ iyalpum ituvākum;
   eṉṟu-eṉpatāka, muṉi etuviṉ aruḷum- muṉivaṉ ētuvōṭu uraitta, moḻi
   avaiyavaikaḷ niṉaiyā-moḻikaḷiṉ poruḷai avvavvakaiyil eṇṇi, vitu vitu
   vitirttu-mey mikavum naṭuṅka, akam nekiḻntu- maṉamkaraintu,
   mikaccōrā-mikavum cōrvuṟṟu, matu malarkoḷ maṇimuṭiya
   maṉṉavaṉ-tēṉporuntiya pūmālaiyaṇinta maṇimuṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya yacōmati,
   maruṇṭāṉ-ceyvakai teriyātu maruḷuvāṉāyiṉaṉ e-ṟu.
   ituveṉpataṉai viḷaivukkum kūṭṭuka. iyalpu, ākupeyar. ētu, etuveṉak
   kuṟuki niṉṟatu. ētu kūṟavē, ataṉōṭiyai puṭaiya eṭuttukkāṭṭum koḷka.
   coṟpalkutal paṟṟi avaṟṟaik kūṟārāyiṉār. viṉaivakaiyum avai
   kāraṇamākavarum kativakaiyum, katitōṟum uḷavākum tuṉpavakaiyum, "avai
   yavaikaḷ niṉaiyā" eṉṟum, niṉaintavaḻi, tāṉceyta viṉaikaḷum, avaṟṟāl
   viḷaiyakkaṭava tuṉpaṅkaḷum tōṉṟavē, yacōmatikkup pēraccam tōṉṟi, avaṉ
   maṉattiṇmaiyai yaḻittamaiyiṉ, uṭal naṭuṅki uḷam karaintu niṉai viḻantu
   mayaṅkaluṟṟāṉ eṉpār, vitu vitu vitirttu akam nekiḻntu mikaccōrā...
   maruṇṭāṉ" eṉṟār. "vitu vitu vitirttu" eṉṟa iraṭṭaikkiḷavi avaṉatu

   makkaḷ iruvarum muṉivaṉpāl varutal
   iṉṉavakai maṉṉaṉmuṉi yiyampiyatu kēḷāt
       taṉṉaruku niṉṟavoru caṇṭaṉai viṭuppa
       maṉṉapaya vuruciyoṭu matiyavaḷum vantē
       coṉṉavi laruṭkuravaṉ tuṇaiyaṭi paṇintār.         301

   urai:- muṉi iṉṉavakai iyampiyatu-cutatta muṉivaṉ ivvāṟu colliyatai,
   maṉṉaṉ kēḷā-vēntaṉāṉa yacōmati kēṭṭu, taṉ aruku niṉṟa oru
   caṇṭaṉai-taṉakku aṇmaiyil oruvaṉāy niṉṟa caṇṭakarumaṉai, viṭuppa-taṉ
   makkaḷaik koṇarumāṟu viṭuttāṉāka, apaya uruciyoṭu matiyavaḷum
   vantu-apayaruciyum apayamatiyākiya appeṇ makaḷum avviṭattiṟku vantu,
   col navil aruḷ kuravaṉ- aṟavuraikaḷaiyē collum aruḷuṭaiyavaṉāṉa cutatta
   muṉivaṉuṭaiya, tuṇaiyaṭi paṇintār-iraṇṭu tiruvaṭikaḷaiyum vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ
   e-ṟu
   maṉamaruṇṭu niṉṟa vēntaṉ teḷiveytiya kālattu muṉivaṉ uraittavaṟṟai
   aiyaviṉākkaḷ pala toṭuttu maṟupaṭiyum naṉku uḷam teḷiyak kēṭṭamai
   tōṉṟa"iṉṉavakai maṉṉaṉ muṉi yiyampiyatu kēḷā" eṉṟu peyarttum kūṟiṉār
   pōlum. kēṭṭavaṉ, yacōtaraṉum cantiramatiyum taṉakku makkaḷāyp
   piṟantiruppatu teḷintatōṭamaiyātu avarkaḷum acceytiyai muṉivaṉpāl
   kēṭṭalvēṇṭumeṉṟa viruppāl avarkaḷai yaḻaittuvarumāṟu caṇṭakarumaṉai
   viṭuttāṉeṉka.maṉ, acai. apayaruci mūttōṉātaliṉ, oruviṉaiyoṭu uyarpiṉ
   vaḻittāy vantatu. vantu eṉṉum ciṟappuviṉai paṇintāreṉṉum potuviṉaiyoṭu

   viḷaiyāṭupa" eṉṟāṟpōla. colleṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, pukaḻum
   puṇṇiyamum payakkum aṟavuraiyeṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu.

       tāṅkalarkaḷ niṉṟu*tava aracaṉaru ḷālē
       nīṅkiyapa vaṅkaḷai niṉaintaṉa ruṇarntār
       āṅkavarka ḷuṟukavalai yāvarpiṟa raṟivār.         302

       (pāṭam) *ceṉṟu.

   urai:- āṅku-avviṭattē, apayavuruciyuṭaṉ apayamati tāṉum-apayaruciyum
   apayamatiyumākiya iruvarum, tāṅkalarkaḷ niṉṟu-mikkeḻum aṉpiṉait
   tāṅkamāṭṭātu toḻutu niṉṟu, tava aracaṉ aruḷāl-tavavēntaṉāṉa cutatta
   muṉivaṉ aruḷiya aruṇ moḻiyāl, nīṅkiya pavaṅkaḷai niṉaintaṉa
   ruṇarntār-kaḻinta piṟappukkaḷai niṉaintu avaṟṟiṟ kētuvākiya tam
   viṉaiyaiyum uṇarntu, avarkaḷ uṟu kavalai-avvuṇarvāl avarkaḷ uṟṟa
   varuttattai, āṅku-appoḻutu, piṟar yāvar aṟivār- avarai yaṉṟip piṟar
   evarum aṟiya māṭṭār e-ṟu.
   muṉivaṉaikkaṇṭa māttiraiyē avartam uḷḷatteḻunta aṉpu avarāl
   tāṅkamāṭṭāvakaiyiṟ perukiyatu tōṉṟa, "tāṅkalarkaḷ" eṉṟum, avar piṉpu
   aṉpiṉ ārāmaiyāl maṟupaṭiyum kaitoḻutu niṉṟārāka, muṉivaṉaruḷāl muṉṉaip
   piṟappuṇarum ñāṉam piṟakka ataṉ tuṇaiyāl avaṟṟai niṉain taṟinta
   nīrmaiyiṉai "niṉṟu tava varacaṉaruḷālē nīṅkiya pavaṅkaḷai niṉaintaṉa
   ruṇarntār" eṉṟār. tavamuṭaiyōr ellōrkkum talaimainilaimai yuṭaimai
   paṟṟic cutattaṉai "tavavaracaṉ" eṉṟār. iṉi, vēntaṉāyiruntu tavamēṟkoṇṭu
   māmuṉivaṉāṉatu paṟṟit tavavaracaṉ eṉṟāreṉṟum kūṟuvar; poruḷaṉṟeṉat
   tuṟanta araciṉait tuṟavātu annilaimaiyum tōṉṟak kūṟuvatiṟ payaṉiṉmaiyiṉ
   atu poruḷaṉmai yuṇarka, tāṅkalarkaḷ niṉaintaṉar eṉpaṉa muṟṟeccam.
   iruvarum varuntutal
   *makkaḷu ḷiraṭṭai yāka māṟiṉam piṟanta yāmuṉ
       mikkatī viṉaiyā luṟṟa viḷaiviṉai yuṇarntē maiya
       tukkamē toṭara nōṟṟut tuṇaiyaṟan tuṟanta peṟṟi
       ikkati tuṉṉik kaṇṭē miṉikkatik keṉcey vōmē.         303
       --------------
       *ivvaṭi naṉṉūluraiyil caṅkara namaccivāyarāl mēṟkōḷākak
       kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. cū. 351.

   urai:- aiya-aiyaṉē, makkaḷuḷ iraṭṭaiyāka māṟiṉam piṟantayām-makkaḷilē
   iraṭṭaiyarāka māṟip piṟantuḷḷayāṅkaḷ, mikka tīviṉaiyāl uṟṟa
   viḷaiviṉai-mikutiyāyc ceyta tīviṉaiyāl uṇṭākiya tuṉpaṅkaḷai, uṇarantēm-
   ippōtu teḷiya aṟintōm, tukkam toṭara nōṟṟu - tuṉpam piṟavitōṟum
   toṭarantu vantu varuttumāṟu tīviṉai ceytu, tuṇaiyaṟam tuṟantu peṟṟi -
   uyirkkut tuṇaiyāy iṉpam payakkum aṟaviṉaiyaic ceyyā toḻinta kuṟṟattai,
   ikkati tuṉṉik kaṇṭōm- ippiṟappai yeyti yaṟintukoṇṭōm, iṉik katikku- iṉi
   varakkaṭava piṟappukku, eṉ ceyvōm - yātu ceyvōm aruḷuka e-ṟu.
   puḷḷum vilaṅkamāyp pala piṟavikaḷilum palavāṟu māṟip piṟanta iruvarum
   ippōtu apayaruciyum apayamatiyumeṉṟa iraṭṭaiyarāyp piṟantiruppatai
   yuṇarntu kūṟaliṉ, "makkaḷuḷ iraṭṭaiyāka māṟiṉam piṟanta yām" eṉṟum,
   tavamuṉivaṉ aruḷāl iṟanta piṟappiṉ tuṉpaṅkaḷai yuṇarntu varuntuvār,
   "muṉmikka tīviṉaiyāl uṟṟa viḷaiviṉai yuṇarntōm" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. viḷaivu,
   tuṉpam; tīviṉaiyiṉ viḷaivu atuvē yātaliṉ. tukkattuk kētuvākiya
   tīviṉaiyē kaṉṟicceyta tiṟattaip paḻitturaittaliṉ, "tukkamē toṭara
   nōṟṟu" eṉṟār. "mikka nallaṟam viḻuttuṇaiyāvatu"(maṇi. 22:138)
   eṉpavākaliṉ, atu tuṇaiyaṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. peṟṟi, īṇṭuk kuṟṟattiṉmēl
   niṉṟatu. tamakku aṟamuṇaratti naṉṉeṟippaṭuttumāṟu vēṇṭum
   kuṟippiṉarātaliṉ, "iṉikkatik keṉ ceyavōmē" eṉṟār.

       1.maṇi. 22:138.

   tantaiyun tanta tāyu mākiya taḻuvu kātal
       maintaṉu maṭantai tāṉu māṟṟiṭaic cuḻaṉṟa peṟṟi
       cintaiyi ṉiṉaintu nontu tēmpiṉar pulampak kaṇṭu
       konteri yaḻaluḷ vīḻnta koḷkaiya ṉāṉāṉ maṉṉaṉ.         304

   urai:-tantaiyum - tantai yākiyum, tanta tāyumākiya - taṉṉaip
   peṟṟuttanta tāyākiyum, taḻuvu kātal - piṟattaṟ kētuvāyirunta
   uḻuvalaṉpuṭaiya, maintaṉum maṭantaitāṉum - apayaruciyum apayamatiyum,
   māṟṟiṭaic cuḻaṉṟa peṟṟi - piṟappu vakaiyil piṟantu varuntiya tiṟattai,
   cintaiyil niṉaintu nontu - tammaṉattē yeṇṇi varunti, tēmpiṉar pulampa -
   tēmpiyaḻavē, maṉṉaṉ kaṇṭu-vēntaṉākiya yacōmati pārttu, kontu eri aḻaluḷ
   vīḻnta koḷkaiyaṉāṉāṉ-kottāka mikkeḻukiṉṟa tīyiṭai vīḻntu varuntum
   varuttattai eytiṉāṉ e-ṟu
   yāṭāyp piṟanta kālattil cantiramatiyum āṭāyp piṟantu uḻaluvāḷaik
   kūṭiyataṉāl, apayaruciyait "tantaiyāy" eṉṟum, akkūṭṭattā luṇṭākiya
   karuviṉkaṇ tāṉē kalantu ataṉ kuṭṭiyāyp piṟantataṉāl avaṉukkut
   tāyāyiṉamaiyiṉ apayamatiyait "tanta tāyum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "taṉṉaiyīṉṟa
   vattāymicait tāḻntatē" (187) eṉṟum, "tāy vayiṟ karuvuṭ ṭakarāyatu"
   (189) eṉṟum varutal kāṇka. iruvarum iraṭṭaiyarāyp piṟattaṟkuk kāraṇam
   iruvarpālum irunta kātalaṉ peṉpār "taḻuvu kātal maintaṉum
   maṭantaitāṉum" eṉṟār. maṭantai, peṇpāṟpeyar; paruvappeyaraṉṟu. māṟṟu;
   piṟavineṟi. mutti neṟiyai nōkka, itu māṟu paṭutaliṉ, māṟṟu eṉappaṭṭatu.
   peṟṟi, taṉmai. muṉṉaip piṟavikaḷil tāmuṟṟa tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum, ceyta
   viṉaikaḷaiyum niṉaintu varuntiyaḻutaṉa reṉṟaṟku "tēmpiṉar pulampa"
   eṉṟum, cintaiyil niṉaintavaḻi muṉṉaip piṟavi varalāṟu tōṉṟi āṇṭu
   nikaḻntavaṟṟai maṉakkaṇṇil kāṭṭi ataṉai nekiḻvittalāl, "cintaiyil
   niṉaintu nontu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. taṉ makkaḷiruvarum maṉam cōrntu
   tēmpippulampak kaṇṭa yacōmatiyiṉ karuttil avaṉceyta tīviṉaikaḷum
   piṟavum tōṉṟi yaccuṟuttavē avaṉ peritum varuntaluṟṟā ṉeṉpār. "konteri
   yaḻaluḷ vīḻnta koḷkaiyaṉāṉāṉ" eṉṟār.
   yacōmati pulamputal
   entaiyum entai tāyum eytiya piṟavi tōṟum
       ventuyar viḷaivu ceyta viṉaiyiṉē ṉeṉcey kēṉō
       antami luyirkaṇ māya valaipala ceytu nāḷum
       ventuyar narakiṉ vīḻkkum viṉaiceytē ṉeṉcey kēṉō.         305

   urai:- entaiyum-eṉ tantaiyākiya yacōtaraṉum, entai tāyum-avaṉ tāyākiya
   cantiramatiyum, eytiya piṟavitōṟum-aṭainta piṟappukkaḷilellām, ventuyar
   viḷaivu ceyta viṉaiyiṉēṉ eṉ ceykēṉ - vevviya tuṉpamuṇṭākkum
   tīviṉaikaḷaic ceytēṉātaliṉ yāṉ iṉi ceyvēṉ, antam il uyirkaḷ māya - aḷa
   villāta uyiriṉaṅkaḷ iṟakkumāṟu, alaipala nāḷum ceytu - tuṉpaṅkaḷ
   palavaṟṟaiyum nāṭōṟum ceytu, ventu uyā narakiṉ vīḻkkum viṉaiceytēṉ -
   tīyil ventu nīṅkiyuyya muṭiyāta narakattiṟ celuttum tīviṉai
   ceytēṉātaliṉ, eṉ ceykēṉ - ivviṉaikaḷiṉāl varakkaṭava tuṉpattiṉ poruṭṭu
   yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ e - ṟu.
   yacōtaraṉ āṭāyppiṟantu vaḷarntu varukaiyil vēṭṭai mēṟ ceṉṟa yacōmati
   koṉṟatum, cantiramati erumaiyāyp piṟanta kālattil taṉ kutiraiyaik
   koṉṟatu kuṟittuk kolai ceytatum piṟavum niṉaintu varuntutaliṉ, "
   piṟavitōṟum ventuyar viḷaivuceyta viṉaiyiṉēṉ" eṉṟum, ivvāṟē tāṉ
   palavuyirkaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭik koṉṟatai niṉaintu "antamil uyirkaḷ māya
   alaipala ceytu" eṉṟum, ittakaiya tīviṉaikaḷāl taṉakku mīḷā narakamē
   kiṭaikkumeṉṟu añci, ventu uyar narakiṉ vīḻkkum viṉai ceytēṉ" eṉṟum
   kūṟippulampiṉāṉ. ivviṉai mikutiyāl viḷaiyum narakattuṉpattiṉiṉṟum
   taṉṉaik kāttuk koḷvatu karuti itupōtu eṇṇat toṭaṅkiṉamai tōṉṟa
   "eṉceykēṉō" eṉṟā ṉeṉkiṉṟār. uyyā narakam, mīḷānarakam. tuṉpattai
   yuṇṭupaṇṇutaliṉ, viṉaiyaiyē viṉai mutalākkik kūṟiṉār.
   aruḷoṭu paṭartal ceyyā tāruyirk kaḻivu ceytē
       poruḷoṭu pōka mēvip poṟiyilē ṉeṉcey kēṉō
       aruḷiṉa turuva māya vaṭikaṇum maṭikaṭ kēyun
       teruḷalaṉ niṉainta tīmaic ciṟiyaṉē ṉeṉcey kēṉō.         306

   urai:-- aruḷoṭu paṭartal ceyyātu - uyirkaḷ māṭṭu aruḷ ceyya niṉaiyātu,
   ār uyirkku - peṟutaṟkariya avvuyirkaṭku, aḻivu ceytu - kēṭṭiṉai
   viḷaivittu, poruḷoṭu pōkam mēvi - poruḷum atu vāyilākap peṟum pōkamumē
   virumpi, poṟiyilēṉ - nalviṉaip pēṟṟukkuriya aṟiviṉṟi yoḻinta yāṉ,
   aruḷiṉatu uruvamāya - aruḷaṟamē uruvākavuṭaiya, aṭikaḷ num aṭikaṭkēyum
   - cāṉṟōrākiya taṅkaṭkum, tīmai niṉainta - tīṅku ceyya niṉainta,
   teruḷalaṉ - teruṇṭa aṟivilēṉāy, ciṟiyēṉ-ciṟumaiyuṟṟēṉātaliṉ,eṉ ceykēṉ-
   ikkuṟṟaṅkaṭkellām parikāram yātu ceyya vallēṉ e-ṟu.
   poṟuḷmēlum pōkattiṉmēlum ceṉṟa eṇṇamikutiyāl evvuyirkkum tīṅkē
   ceytoḻikiṉē ṉeṉpāṉ, "aruḷoṭu paṭartal ceyyātu" eṉṟum, "poruḷoṭu pōkam
   mēvi" yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. "yāam irappavai, poruḷum poṉṉum pōkamulla, aruḷum
   aṉpum aṟaṉum" 1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. paṭartal, niṉaintoḻukal.
   āruyir eṉṟāṉ, uyirkaḷiṉ arumaiyiṉai muṉṉar uṇarātiruntu ippōtu uṇarntu
   kūṟumāṟu tōṉṟa. taṉpāl aruḷillāmaiyiṉ mikutikku ellai yituveṉṟaṟku,
   "aruḷuruvāya aṭikkaṭkum tīṅku niṉaintēṉ" eṉṟāṉ. yāṉ aruḷā toḻiyiṉum
   aruḷuruvāya taṅkaṭkēṉum tīmai niṉaiyātirukkum ciṟṟaṟivum peṟṟilē
   ṉeṉpāṉ, "teruḷalaṉ tīmai naṉainta ciṟiyaṉēṉ" eṉṟāṉ. aṭikaḷāya nuṅkaṭku
   eṉṟaṟku, "aṭikaḷ num aṭikaṭku" eṉṟatu naṟcīlam. cālpuṭaimaiyāṟ periya
   rāyiṉārai muṉṉilaippaṭutti nīvir mutaliya coṟkaḷāl colvatu cīlamaṉṟu
   eṉpar. itu vaḻakkiṉākiya uyarcol. eṉaceykēṉē eṉap paṉmuṟaiyum kūṟiyatu,
   avaṉatu kaiyaṟaviṉaip pulappaṭutti niṉṟatu.

       1. pari.5.

   muṉivaṉai vēṇṭal
   māviyal vaṭivu taṉṉai vataiceytār vaṇṇa mītēl
       āviṉi* yaḷiya ṉētum añcilē ṉavati yeṉkol
       kāvala varuḷu keṉṉak kalaṅkiṉa ṉaracaṉ vīḻa
       māvala añca leṉṟam mātava ṉuraiva ḷarttāṉ.         307
       --------
       (pāṭam.) * āvinī.

   urai:-kāvala-uyirkaṭku aruḷaicceytu aṟattaik kākkum tavavēntē, mā iyal
   vaṭivu taṉṉai vatai ceytār vaṇṇam - māvāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatoru kōḻi
   vaṭiviṉaik koṉṟavar aṭainta tuṉpam, ītēl-it taṉmait tākumāyiṉ,
   aḷiyaṉ-aḷikkattakka yāṉ, ētum añci lēṉ-ciṟitum añcātu tīmai
   purintēṉātaliṉ, iṉi avati eṉkol-ippōtu eṉakku eytavirukkum tuṉpattukku
   ellai yātō aṟiyēṉ. aruḷuka- aṭiyēṉukku aruḷ ceyyavēṇṭum, eṉṉa-eṉṟu,
   aracaṉ kalaṅkiṉaṉ vīḻa-aracaṉākiya yacōmati uṇarvu kalaṅki muṉivaṉ
   tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉāka. am mātavaṉ-ataṉaikkaṇṭa ammuṉivaṉ,
   māvala-kutirai celuttavalla vēntē, añcal-iṉi nī añcaṟka, eṉṟu urai
   vaḷarttāṉ-eṉṟu collaluṟṟāṉ e-ṟu.
   mā iyalvaṭivu-māvāl ceyyappaṭṭa kōḻiyiṉ uruvam. "māviṉāl vaṉainta kōḻi
   vaṭivu"(145) eṉṟē muṉpum kūṟiṉar. mākkōḻiyai yacōtaraṉ taṉ kaivāḷāl
   arinta pōtu, ataṉ talai kūvikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu vīḻntamaiyiṉālum, avvāṟu
   ceyyumāṟu ceyvittavaḷ cantiramati yātalālum iruvaraiyum muṟaiyē
   ceytōrum ceyvittārumākiya viṉai mutalākki "vatai ceytār" eṉṟār.
   uyirkkōḻi pōlak kūvi vīḻntamaiyāl ataṉ kolaiyiṉai "vatai" yeṉṟār. ak
   kolaiviṉai kāraṇamākap palvakaip piṟappum piṟantu iru varum tuṉpuṟṟa
   ceytiyaic cutatta muṉivaṉ kūṟak kēṭṭāṉātaliṉ "vaṇṇam ītēl" eṉṟāṉ.
   uyiril poruḷoṉṟaṉai uyiruṭaiyatākak karuticceyta kolaiviṉaiyē ittuṇait
   tuṉ pattaip payakkumāyiṉ, uyiruḷ poruḷkaḷaiyē kolai purinta taṉṉuṭaiya
   tīviṉaikaḷai niṉaintu varuntum yacōmati, uyirk kolaiyait tavirttal
   karuti māvāl kōḻiceytu koṉṟa avariṉum, kolai viṉaiyiṉ tīmaiyai
   niṉaiyātē ceyta taṉakku ettuṇait tuṉpa meytumōveṉa añci naṭuṅki "ā iṉi
   aḷiyaṉ ētum añcilēṉ avati yeṉkol" eṉṟāṉ. ā, irakkakkuṟippu. uyirkaṭkuc
   ceytaṟkuri aruḷaṟa mēṟkoṇṭoḻukuvatu karuti, cutattaṉai "kāvala" eṉṟum,
   taṉakku ak kolaiviṉaiyaip pōkkutaṟkuk kaḻuvāy aruḷumāṟu vēṇṭum
   karuttiṉāl "aruḷuka" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. māvalaṉ, kutirai, yāṉai mutaliya
   paṭaikaḷaic celuttum vaṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉ.
   "māvalōṉ" (19) eṉap piṟāṇṭum kūṟutal kāṇka. iṉi, ivaiyiraṇṭaṟkum mikka
   veṟṟiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟalumoṉṟu. uraivaḷarttā ṉeṉpatu vāḷā
   uraittāṉeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu.
   cutattaṉ vēntaṉait teruṭṭutal
   aṟivila rāya kālat taṟivila ceyta ellām
       neṟiyiṉi laṟiva tūṟa niṉṟaṉar* vilaki niṟpar
       aṟiyalar viṉaika ḷālē yarunavai paṭunar kaiyāṟ†
       ciṟiyanal vataṅkaḷ ceytē tīviṉai yakalvar kāṇāy‡         308
       ----------------
       (pāṭam) *niṉṟavai. † paṭunarkkaiyā. ‡ ceyta tiruviṉai numarkaḷ
       kāṇāy.

   urai:- aṟivilarāya kālattu-nallaṟivu piṟavāta iḷamaik kālattil, aṟivila
   ceyta ellām-aṟiyāmaiyāṟ ceyta tīmaikaḷellām, aṟivatu neṟiyiṉil ūṟa
   niṉṟaṉar-nallaṟivāṉatu neṟippaṭi uṇṭākap peṟṟavar, vilaki niṟpar-at
   tīmaiyiṉiṉṟum vilaki naṉṉeṟik kaṇṇē niṟpārkaḷ.aṟiyalarviṉaikaḷālē aru
   navai paṭunar-nalantīṅku aṟiyārāycceyta viṉaikaḷāl nīṅkutaṟkariya
   tuṉpappaṭuvar, kaiyāl -tam ceykaikaḷāl, ciṟiya nal vataṅkaḷ ceytu-eḷiya
   virataṅkaḷ palavaṟṟai mēṟkoṇṭoḻuki, tīviṉai akalvar-tīviṉiyiṉiṉṟum
   nīṅkuvārkaḷ e-ṟu
   aṟivillāk kālattē ceyyum tīviṉai palavum aṟivil ceykaikaḷākavum,
   nalviṉaiyum ēraleḻuttup pōlvatōr viḻukkāṭeṉa nallōrāl
   koḷḷappaṭāmaiyālum iruvakaiyum cēra. "aṟivilaceyta vellām" eṉṟār. neṟi,
   kalvi kēḷvi neṟi. nallaṟivu piṟattaṟku ivaiyiraṇṭum neṟiyātaliṉ, "neṟi"
   yeṉṟār. aṟivatu, atu, pakutipporuḷ vikuti. ūṟutal, uṇṭātal. "kaṟṟaṉait
   tūṟu maṟivu"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. nallaṟivu piṟantavaḻit
   tīviṉai tavirttu nalviṉaiyē nāṭic ceyyappaṭutaliṉ, "vilaki niṟpar"
   eṉṟār,. iṉi aṟivu piṟavākkālattē ceyta viṉaippayaṉ tīrtaṟkuk kaḻuvāy
   kūṟuvār , nalloḻukkamum eḷiya viratamum mēṟkoḷva teṉpār "ciṟiyanal
   vataṅkaḷ kaiyāl ceytē tīviṉai yakalvar" eṉṟār. ciṟumai, eḷimai mēṟṟu.
   aṟiyāmaiyāṟ ceytaṉa vāyiṉum tīviṉaippayaṉāy varum tuṉpam nīkkutaṟkaritu
   eṉpār "arunavai" eṉṟum, eṉavē, viṉaippayaṉai evvāṟṟāṉum nukarntē
   kaḻittalvēṇṭum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāreṉa vaṟika. aṟivilāk kālattuc ceyta kuṟṟam
   aṟivaṟiyumpōtu aṟivuṭaiyōrāl vilakkappaṭumeṉpatum, akkālattucceyta
   viṉaippayaṉkaḷ aṟivaṟivār mēṟkoḷḷum vataṅkaḷāl vellappaṭum eṉpatum
   kūṟiyavāṟām.

       1. kuṟaḷ 396

   iṟaivaṉ aṟattiṉ ēṟṟattaik kūṟutal
   aruḷpuri maṉatta rāki yāruyirk kapaya nalkip
       poruḷkolai kaḷavu kāmam poyyivai puṟakka ṇittiṭ
       ṭiruḷpuri viṉaikaḷ cērā tiṟaivaṉa taṟattai yeytiṉ
       maruḷceya varuvatuṇṭō vāṉava riṉpa mallāl.         309

   urai:- aruḷpuri maṉattarāki-aruḷē niṉaiyum maṉamuṭaiyavarāy, ār
   uyirkku-niṟainta uyirkaṭkellām, apayam nalki-ātaravu ceytu, poruḷ kolai
   kaḷavu kāmam poy ivai puṟakkaṇittiṭṭu-piṟaṉ poruḷai virumputalum
   kolaiyum kaḷavum kāmamum poy kūṟalum eṉṟa ivaiyiṟṟaic ceyyārāy,
   iruḷpuri viṉaikaḷ cērātu-aṟiyāmaiyāṟ piṟakkum viṉaikaḷaic ceyvatiṉṟi,
   iṟaivaṉatu aṟattai eytiṉ-arukaparamaṉ aruḷiya aṟattai mēṟkoṇ
   ṭoḻukuvārāyiṉ, vāṉavar iṉpamallāl-tēvarulaka viṉpamaṉṟi, maruḷ ceya
   varuvatu -oruvarkku mayakkamum atu kāraṇamāka varum narakat tuṉpamum
   ceyya varuvatoṉṟu illaiyām e-ṟu
   arukaṉ aruḷiya aṟaṅkaḷuḷ talaiyāyatu aruḷātaliṉ, ataṉai vitantu
   "aruḷpuri maṉattarāki" yeṉṟum ataṟkuriya ceyalātaliṉ, "āruyirk kapayam
   nalki" yeṉṟum, avvaṟattaik keṭuppaṉavātaliṉ poruḷveḵkal mutaliyavaṟṟaip
   "puṟakkaṇittiṭṭu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "iruḷcēr iru viṉai"1 eṉṟārākaliṉ, aḵtu
   ic camaṇ camayattukkum otta karuttātalpaṟṟi, "iruḷpuri viṉaikaḷ cērātu"
   eṉṟum, viṉaippakai vīyātu piṉceṉ ṟaṭuvatupaṟṟi ataṟku araṇ iḵtu eṉpār,
   iṟaivaṉa taṟattai araṇākavum kūṟiṉār. "eytiṉ" eṉṟatu eytiṉā leytum
   payaṉ kūṟutaṟku avāynilai payappittatu. maruḷ, piṟavikkētuvāya
   mayakkam.
